# OutRunners (MAME set 'orunners', World) - V70 program symbols
#
# Addresses and names only: no game data lives in this file.
# Format:  ADDRESS  KIND  NAME  [LAYOUT]   [# comment]
#   KIND: func = function entry, code = code label, data = data label
#   A code label of a computed call or jump may say where it goes: calls=TABLE, the code of a described table
#   register RN NAME: a register the whole program shares, a global variable of the C (its name)
# Addresses use the copy of the program the code refers to:
#   000000-03FFFF (ic37 + ic40, a 16-bit word of each in every 32-bit word), 100000-1FFFFF (ic36 + ic39)
# Mirrors are accepted and folded (e.g. FFFFF0 -> 03FFF0).
#
# LAYOUT, for data tables that hold code addresses the analysis cannot see the code load:
#   record=N        the table is an array of N-byte records
#   count=N         ... of N records, or
#   count=prefix    ... of as many records as the 16-bit word before them says, or
#   count=prefix32  ... of as many records as the 32-bit word before them says, or
#   count=bytes32   ... of as many records as fill the length in bytes the 32-bit word before them says, or
#   end=XXXX        ... closed by this 16-bit word where the next record would start
#   kinds=O:K=N,..  ... of records whose length the 16-bit field at offset O picks: N bytes for kind K
#                   (hexadecimal), 'end' for a kind that closes the array, or 'fork' for one that closes it
#                   with a table of addresses of arrays that go on from there (as many as fit before the
#                   first of them)
#   code=A,B        every record holds a 32-bit code address at these offsets (0 = no code);
#                   A:BASE is a 16-bit offset from BASE instead
#   data=A,B        every record holds at these offsets a 32-bit value that its code may take as the address
#                   of data: the data it goes with, when the value's low 24 bits (the V70's address bus) are
#                   an address of the program ROM that is not code (0 and numbers are not)
#   lists=N         the table is N 16-bit offsets, from its own address, to such arrays
#   pointers=N      the table is N 32-bit addresses of such arrays (0: no array)
# Sizes, offsets and counts are decimal; end and BASE are hexadecimal, like the data and addresses.

# ======== entry points ========
03FFF0  func  reset              # V70 reset vector (0xFFFFFFF0 on a 24-bit bus): "jmp 6"
comment reset The V70's reset vector, at the top of the program's address space: a jump to boot.
000006  func  boot               # sets SBR to 03F000, the stack to 21FC00, and copies blocks to E00000 and E80000
03F000  data  vector_table       # 256 x 32-bit entries, base loaded into SBR by boot
03F8B4  func  int_default        # target of 250 of the 256 vector table entries
03F8C0  func  int_vector_02      # NMI
# The V70 takes maskable interrupts at vector 0x40 + the number the interrupt controller (D00000) supplies
03F500  func  int_vector_40
03F794  func  int_vector_41
03F7A0  func  int_vector_42
03F7AC  func  int_vector_43
03F830  func  int_vector_44

# ======== the registers the program shares ========
# Globals of the C (decompiler/signatures.py CONTEXT); each task has its own copy, which the runtime swaps
# with it. R12, R14, R26, R27 and R28 are shared too, as scratch registers whose last value the next
# function may read (a handler what the one before it left), and keep their register names
register R20  current_object     # the object the object loop (011CC0) runs: set before it calls the handler at field 4
register R23  player_car         # the screen's car: the object at 1000[R25] (209000 or 219000)
register R25  screen             # the block of the screen the task runs: 208000 for screen 1 (the second task), 218000 for screen 2 (the boot task)
register R32  stack_pointer      # SP, in the functions whose stack stays in memory (decompiler/stack.py); 21F400 after boot

# ======== structures ========
# The game's own (decompiler/pointers.py works out which registers point at them): the objects, 124 in the
# page of each screen's block, run by the object loop; the screen's car block, at 1000 of its block (its
# field 0 is the car's object, which player_car_update runs); the block of the screen a task runs (R25).
# Times are in the task's frames: each task runs every other video frame, 30 a second
struct object 100
struct car 200
struct screen 8000
# Each player's race record, two copies of 0x80 bytes in the game's variables: 20FB00 and 20FB80 (the number
# of stages at 60, the stage at 62, the time of the last checkpoint pass at 68, the time left at 6A in 30ths
# of a second, the race time at 6C in thousandths), and the copy at 20FC00 and 20FC80 each screen keeps
struct race 80
# Below each screen's block (screen - 8000), its objects' area: this frame's list of objects and the next
# frame's (each a count, then the low halves of the objects' addresses; the two, at 200 and 300, swap every
# frame), the used slots (7C bytes at 10, FF used), a spare object at 100 and the 124 objects from 400
struct object_area 8000
struct object_list 100
field object_area 00 list u32 object_list            # this frame's list, which run_objects walks
field object_area 04 list_first u32 object_list      # its first object's place (nothing reads it)
field object_area 08 next_list u32 object_list       # the next frame's list, where run_objects puts the live ones
field object_area 0C list_end u32 object_list        # past this frame's list's last object (nothing reads it)
field object_area 10 slots u8                        # the first of the 7C slots: FF used, 0 free
field object_list 00 count u16
field object 08 slot u8                # its slot in the objects' area (start_object), which run_objects frees when it dies
field object 00 flags u8               # bit 0: the object is live (run_objects runs it; else it frees its slot)
field object 04 handler u32            # the code run_objects runs for it each frame ("jsr [4[R20]]", 011CD2)
field object 0C state u8               # the step its handler is at: most handlers go by it through a table
# How draw_object draws it (by draw_mode, through rom_00EB44): an animation of the animations (rom_034138),
# each a list of frames, each a list of pieces; the palette word (rom_032BA4); flags bits 6-7 flip it
field object 30 x u16                  # draw mode 0: its place on the screen, less the screen's scroll (screen 14, 16)
field object 32 y u16
field object 34 width_scale u16        # the pieces' width on the screen (sprite word 3) = their size * this >> 8
field object 36 height_scale u16       # ... and their height (sprite word 2)
field object 38 animation u16          # the animation it shows (rom_034138)
field object 3A palette u16            # its palette (rom_032BA4: the sprites' palette words)
field object 3E draw_mode u8           # how draw_object places it: 0 on the screen at x, y; others on the road
field object 40 frame u8               # the frame of its animation
field object 43 size_step u8           # the row of piece sizes its pieces take (0x122B74, 3 a frame piece)
field object 44 scale_shift u8         # the road modes shift the road's scale left by it
field screen 20 race_copy u32 race     # 20FC00 for screen 1, 20FC80 for screen 2
field screen 24 race u32 race          # 20FB00 for screen 1, 20FB80 for screen 2

# ======== the frame loop ========
# 0008F0-000912: the program waits for the next frame polling 23[R24] (20E023), which an interrupt sets,
# and clears it; while B00[R25] says data is being unpacked it calls 010E42 for a piece of it each time
000908  code  frame_wait_loop    # "test.b 23[R24]": the poll
00090D  code  frame_wait_done    # the wait is over: a frame is done

# ======== the tasks ========
# The program runs two tasks, each on a stack of its own: the one boot starts runs the loop of screen 2
# (R25 = 218000, 000719), the other the loop of screen 1 (R25 = 208000, 0006B0). Boot (0005C5-0005EF)
# copies the second one's first interrupt frame (registers, PC 0006B0, PSW 00040000) from 000C98 to
# 21FF84, and keeps its stack pointers at 0[R24] (the frame) and 4[R24] (21F800, its L0SP). When 24[R24]
# is clear, the VBLANK handler swaps them (03F5FC): it stores the running task's pair there and returns
# into the other one
0006B0  func  second_task        # where the second task's first frame returns to
task  second_task
03F5FC  code  task_swap          # 03F5FC-03F608: the running task's stacks go to 0[R24] and 4[R24], the other one's come back
03F60C  code  task_swap_done     # the handler goes on with the other task's interrupt frame: it returns into that task

# ======== the objects ========
# Below each screen's block (screen - 8000): this frame's list of objects at -8000 (a count, then the low
# halves of their addresses), the next frame's at -7FF8 (the two lists, at -7E00 and -7D00, swap every
# frame), the used slots at -7FF0 (7C bytes, FF used), a spare object at -7F00 and the 124 objects from
# -7C00, 100 bytes each
011CC0  func  run_objects        # the object loop: each live object of this frame's list (flags bit 0) has its handler run and goes on the next frame's list; a dead one frees its slot; then this frame's list is cleared and the two swap
011D43  func  start_object       # the first free slot: its 100 bytes cleared, live (flags bit 0), its slot number at 8, on this frame's list; R21 = the object (the spare at screen - 7F00 when no slot is free)
comment run_objects The object loop, once a frame for the screen: each live object (flags bit 0) of this frame's list (screen - 8000) has its handler run and goes on the next frame's list (screen - 7FF8); a dead one frees its slot (its byte at screen - 7FF0, by its slot number at 8). Then this frame's list is cleared and the two lists swap.
comment start_object Takes the first free object slot of the screen (FF into its byte at screen - 7FF0), clears its 100 bytes, makes it live (flags bit 0) with its slot number at 8, puts it on this frame's list and returns it; with no slot free, it returns the spare object at screen - 7F00 without any of that.

# ======== object handler lists ========
# A 32-bit count, then that many handlers: the code takes a list with "movea.b list[PC], R10" and starts an
# object per handler with "mov.w [R10+], R8" (the count) and "mov.w [R10+], 4[R21]" (011E2C-011E35); the
# object loop calls the handler at field 4 (011CD2, "jsr [4[R20]]")
003470  data  object_handlers_346C  record=4 count=1 code=0    # the count at 00346C; list taken at 003133
003478  data  object_handlers_3474  record=4 count=19 code=0   # the count at 003474; list taken at 003161
0034C8  data  object_handlers_34C4  record=4 count=9 code=0    # the count at 0034C4; list taken at 003170
0034F0  data  object_handlers_34EC  record=4 count=1 code=0    # the count at 0034EC; list taken at 003257
001A7C  data  object_handlers_1A78  record=4 count=3 code=0    # the count at 001A78; list taken at 0014BA
002D5C  data  object_handlers_2D58  record=4 count=4 code=0    # the count at 002D58

# Tables of lists taken by an index: "mov.w table[PC](R0), R10", then the records with [R10+]
014A20  data  object_lists_14A20  pointers=16 record=8 count=prefix32 code=0 data=4   # 0146E9-014709: a 32-bit count, then (handler -> field 4, data -> field 80) pairs
014A60  data  object_list_14A60  record=8 count=prefix32 code=0 data=4    # the same, taken whole at 0146DB
0181B8  data  object_lists_181B8  pointers=62 record=8 count=bytes32 code=0 data=4  # 017BD2-017BF6: the records' length in bytes (the count is >> 3), then (handler, data) pairs
02E1F0  data  object_lists_2E1F0  pointers=32 record=16 end=FFFF8000 code=4 data=12  # 0137F1, 013809-013821: (position, type, handler -> field 4, field 84, field 18) records, closed by 8000 FFFF
00DF78  data  object_handlers_DF74  record=4 count=3 code=0    # the count at 00DF74; list taken at 00DE57
000F74  data  object_handlers_F70  record=4 count=1 code=0     # the count at 000F70: game mode 8's (the ISDN connection)

# The objects of each stage's road: a stream of (position, command) halfword pairs, taken from this table by
# the stage's number (012720 and 0127BF, "mov.w 20800(R0), 80[R20]") and read at 01272C-012746 as the road
# goes by. Command 0 starts an object (handler -> field 4, then a parameter -> field 18, 012758); 1 ends the
# stage's stream, which goes on from this table when the next stage starts (012777, 0127A4-0127CA); 2 ends
# it for good (01279C); 3 is a fork: a table of the streams of its branches follows, and the one the route
# takes, C9[R23], goes on when the stage changes (012786, 0127CD-0127EB)
020800  data  road_object_streams  pointers=32 record=12 kinds=2:0=12,1=end,2=end,3=fork code=4 data=8

# A timeline: (frame, routine) pairs that 01AE0D-01AE2C calls when the object's counter, 10[R20], reaches the
# frame, from the address 01ADFC puts in 80[R20]; closed by frame 0000FFFF
01AFC0  data  timeline_1AFC0  record=8 end=0000FFFF code=4
01AE24  code  timeline_call  calls=timeline_1AFC0    # "jsr [R1]": the routine of the record the counter reached

# The radio DJ's voice lines: (sound << 24 | address) records; the address is a string of mouth shapes
# (indices into 018AE8, pairs of the LCD's custom characters, shown in its characters 0-1) closed by a
# negative byte. start_dj_message (019402) starts line R0 (the sound through request_sound, the string's
# address to F30[R25]); 0189D9 shows its next shape every third frame
019576  data  dj_messages  record=4 count=61 data=0

# 019266-019294: lists of routines an object runs one after another, a step each time; 019280 picks the list
# by a number below 14 and keeps its place at 38[R20]
01966C  data  routine_lists_1966C  pointers=14 record=4 end=00000000 code=0
019294  code  routine_list_jump  calls=routine_lists_1966C   # "jmp [R0]": the list's next routine

# ======== code and data nothing uses ========
# No scenario runs or reads them, and no table or instruction holds their addresses: functions nothing
# calls, code a branch or a jump right before it always goes past, and tables nothing reads. The "func"
# ones are decompiled too. The "code" ones are left out of the C: they jump or call into the middle of
# functions the game runs, and their C would cut those functions in two (tools/coverage.py counts them
# as code all the same)
0002E6  code  unused_0002E6      # unused: what the branch before it skips, then on into the code at 0002EA
0002FD  code  unused_0002FD      # unused: runs into the code at 00041B
000303  code  unused_000303      # unused: what the branch before it skips, then on into the code at 00041B
000310  func  eeprom_read_one_word         # unused: what the branch before it skips
000327  func  reload_settings         # unused: what the branch before it skips
000354  func  eeprom_write_one_word         # unused
000EA4  func  isdn_status_update         # the handler of object_handlers_F70, which game mode 8 (the ISDN connection) starts
0010CD  func  bar_picture_start         # unused: starts objects (011D43)
001737  code  unused_001737      # unused object set-up; its handler (001820) calls into 011EB8 at 011EC0
001783  code  unused_001783      # unused object set-up, like unused_001737
0017CF  code  unused_0017CF      # unused object set-up, like unused_001737
00183B  data  unk_00183B         # unused: bytes 60-67
004578  func  route_map_leg_car_end         # unused: after a jump that always leaves (to 00EAE4)
009EFC  data  unk_009EFC         # unused: 16-bit words 0DC6-0DCD
009FE4  data  unk_009FE4         # unused: 32-bit numbers
00A337  code  unused_00A337      # unused: what the branch before it skips, then on into the code at 00A373
00AB04  data  unk_00AB04  record=4 count=8 code=0   # unused: the addresses of the code after it (00AB24, 00AB36)
00AB66  code  unused_00AB66      # unused: the code of unk_00AB04, on into the code at 00A71B
00C552  func  lap_timer_spare_return         # unused: an rsr after a jump that always leaves (to 00EAE4)
00C8B5  func  stage_entry_spare_return         # unused: an rsr after the function's own rsr
00CEA4  func  time_to_text_thousandths         # unused: what the branch before it skips
00D658  func  network_check_delay         # unused
00D797  code  unused_00D797      # unused: what the branch before it skips, then on into 00D7CB

# ======== road objects (naming batch A) ========
00EAE4  func  draw_object
00F30D  func  animation_advance
00F366  func  animation_start
00F5C8  func  road_row_draw
00F911  func  road_row_pair_draw
0122E3  func  fork_sign_hit_check
0122E8  func  road_row_crash_check
0122EB  func  road_row_hit_check
0123AF  func  road_row_pair_crash_check
01287C  func  scenery_update
012894  func  road_row_start
012906  func  flashing_scenery_update
0129AB  func  scenery_pair_update
0129F4  func  overhead_scenery_update
012ACF  func  fork_sign_update
012AF4  func  animated_scenery_update
012B0C  func  road_row_start_copy
012B84  func  animated_scenery_pair_update
012BAE  func  road_crosser_update
012DB7  func  moving_scenery_update
013104  func  flagman_update
comment scenery_update Static scenery along the road (trees, buildings, crowds, banners, signs): a row of identical sprites from its record. By state: 0 road_row_start; 1 road_row_crash_check, then road_row_draw; 2 (a record without a hit box) road_row_draw only. Ends when road_row_draw finds the whole row behind the view.
comment scenery_pair_update Static scenery drawn twice, at each side of the road, the second mirrored (rows of trees, houses, spectators). By state: 0 road_row_start; 1 road_row_pair_crash_check, then road_row_pair_draw; 2 (no hit box) road_row_pair_draw only.
comment fork_sign_update The signs on the road where it forks at the end of a stage: a line down the middle before the fork and a line across each branch after it. By state: 0 road_row_start; 1 fork_sign_hit_check, then road_row_draw; 2 road_row_draw only. A car that hits one is slowed, not crashed.
comment fork_sign_hit_check road_row_hit_check with kind 3 (R27): a car that hits a fork sign is slowed for 15 frames, as off the road (car slowed_frames), instead of crashing.
comment animated_scenery_update Scenery that animates in place (the Ferris wheel, windmills; also the scenery of the goal lines). By state: 0 road_row_start_copy; 1 road_row_crash_check; 1 and 2 animation_advance and road_row_draw.
comment animated_scenery_pair_update Animated scenery drawn twice, at each side of the road, the second mirrored (the people lining the start and the goal, the scenery around the forks). By state: 0 road_row_start_copy; 1 road_row_pair_crash_check; 1 and 2 animation_advance and road_row_pair_draw.
comment flashing_scenery_update Scenery whose palette cycles a step every frame, for flashing lights (the Christmas trees, the arches over the underwater tunnel, a fairground ride). By state: 0 reads its record as road_row_start does, except that the second and third halfwords are the address of its list of palettes (palette_cycle) instead of a palette; 1 road_row_crash_check; 1 and 2 the next palette from the list into palette (a negative entry goes back that many bytes first), then road_row_draw.
comment overhead_scenery_update Animated scenery high over the road, drawn from the middle of the screen instead of the road's centre line (screen_centered), so it does not swing with the bends: the schools of fish over the underwater tunnel, the cloud bands of stage 25. By state: 0 screen_centered = FF, then road_row_start (its records have no hit box); 1 and 2 animation_advance and road_row_draw.
comment flagman_update The man waving a chequered flag at each fork and at the goal. By state: 0 reads the record (animation, in_view_animation, palette, side, x_offset, y_offset), draw_mode 9, scale_shift 1, appear_position = position - draw_distance (and 58 = position - 30, never read); 1 not drawn until the view (race_copy window_segment) passes appear_position, then in_view_animation (06F, the waving; so 06E is never drawn); 1 and 2: ends once the car (car window_segment) reaches its position, else animation_advance and draw_object.
comment road_crosser_update What walks across the road as the car comes near (the camels of the desert stages). By state: 0 reads the record (animation, crossing_animation, palette, flags, side, x_offset, trigger_distance, x_speed, draw_mode, scale_shift; y_offset -256 for animation 165), trigger_position = position - trigger_distance, appear_position = position - draw_distance; 1 not drawn until the view (race_copy window_segment) passes appear_position; 2 drawn standing (draw_object) until the car (car segment) reaches trigger_position, then crossing_animation; 3 while the car (car window_segment) has not reached it: x_offset += x_speed and position + 1 each frame, and while within draw_distance of the car animation_advance and draw_object; it ends when the car reaches it, it gets beyond draw_distance or its screen_x leaves -F0..130.
comment moving_scenery_update Scenery that travels across the view once the car comes near (helicopters, the roller coaster train, birds, fish over the tunnel, a tanker truck on a bridge). By state: 0 reads the record (animation, palette, flags, draw_mode, scale_shift, side, x_offset, y_offset, trigger_distance + 16, x_speed, y_speed, road_speed), trigger_position = position - trigger_distance; 1 not drawn until the view (race_copy window_segment) passes trigger_position; 1 and 2: while the car (car window_segment) has not reached it, each frame x_offset += x_speed, y_offset += y_speed, position += road_speed, and while within draw_distance of the car animation_advance and draw_object; it ends when the car reaches it, it gets beyond draw_distance or its screen_x leaves -F0..130.
comment road_row_start Sets up a row of identical sprites along the road from its record (record): animation (animation_start), palette, flags bits to set (bit 6 mirrors the sprites), size_table, scale_shift, side, x_offset, y_offset, spacing, count and the hit box (hit_box points at it; none when its first 32 bits are 0); first_position = position, end_position = position + spacing * (count - 1) + 1. Goes on to the next state, or the one after it (no hit check) without a hit box. Used by scenery, scenery_pair, fork_sign and overhead_scenery.
comment road_row_start_copy The same code as road_row_start, at another address: the first step of animated_scenery and animated_scenery_pair.
comment road_row_hit_check Whether the screen's car hits an item of the row, unless the car's object has flags bit 3 set: the car's box (the entry SCREEN(screen)->unk_500 points at: its segments at +6 and +8, along the road +A..+C in 128ths of a segment from its first, across +E..+10) against each item's hit_box (along from (item - the car's first segment) * 128 + position_fraction, across from the road's width * side + x_offset), for the items from first_position to end_position within the car's segments. On the first that overlaps: the car's object gets flags bit 2 and byte 6E = R27, the kind of hit (0 a crash, 3 slowed), and the car's unk_122 and unk_120 half the distances from the car's box to the item's (along, across).
comment road_row_crash_check road_row_hit_check with kind 0 (R27): hitting an item crashes the car. Used by scenery, animated_scenery and flashing_scenery (and 012FBC).
comment road_row_pair_crash_check road_row_hit_check for the rows drawn in mirrored pairs, with kind 0 (a crash): each item's hit box is tested as it is and mirrored across the road's centre. Used by scenery_pair and animated_scenery_pair.
comment road_row_draw Draws the row's items in view: ends the object once end_position is not past the view's start (race_copy window_segment); nothing while first_position is beyond the draw distance (SCREEN(screen)->unk_12 >> 7 segments); moves first_position up to the first item in view; for each item (spacing apart, to the draw distance) where the road has a scale: x = (the road's width * side + x_offset) * scale >> 12 plus the road's screen x unless screen_centered, y = y_offset * scale >> 12 plus the road's screen y; off the screen (x beyond -1E0..1E0, y beyond -160..80) it is skipped, else the scale shifted by scale_shift picks the size (size_table: a size step and a shift) and a sprite of the animation's frame with its palette (flags bits 6-7 flip it) goes into the screen's sprite list (unk_C0; the copy at +3000 is the halfway point from the item's sprite of the frame before, previous_sprites) and the depth list of its road segment (screen + 5000). Sets flags bit 5 and keeps where its sprites went in previous_sprites. Used by scenery, fork_sign, animated_scenery, flashing_scenery and overhead_scenery (and 01351E).
comment road_row_pair_draw road_row_draw for the rows drawn in mirrored pairs: each item twice, at the same distance either side of the road's centre, the second with sprite attribute bits 43 toggled (bit 6 flips it, bits 0-1 swap its X alignment); there is no off-screen test and no screen_centered. Used by scenery_pair and animated_scenery_pair.
comment animation_start Starts animation R9 (rom_034138[R9] is its record) at its first frame: frame_time and frame_countdown from the record's first byte, size_step 0, and fields 34 and 36 to 100 (the width and height zoom of the screen-position drawing, draw_object_on_screen: 100 the sprite's own size).
comment animation_advance Steps the object's animation: nothing while frame_time is 0; when frame_countdown runs out (back to frame_time), the next frame. A frame's entry in the record (32 bits at +4 + frame * 4) is the address of its data when positive, else an order: 0 holds the frame before (frame_time 0), -1 goes back to frame 0, -2 starts the animation whose number is the next entry.
comment draw_object Draws the object's sprite, unless flags bit 1 is set or the screen's sprite list is full: field 3E picks the routine (rom_00EB44): some project the object's road position (1C) with its offsets (20, 24) and set fields 30 and 32 to where it lands and 3C to the road's depth layer there, some draw it at the screen position in 30 and 32, some copy a ready sprite entry from the address in field 38; then links it into the depth list of layer 3C (screen + 5000).

comment lcd_race_info_start Starts the race information on the cabinet LCD: clears it (clear_lcd), shows the DJ's face in its first two characters (screen dj_face) and goes on to lcd_race_info_update.
comment lcd_race_info_update Shows the race on the cabinet LCD. Before the start, while the car object's unk_10 counts the frames to it: a bar shrinking through each second (rom_018EA0) with the seconds left. Then a page every 150 frames (page_frames), by page: 0 Time (lcd_race_info_time), 1 Order (the car numbers, car_numbers + 1, in race order from order_list, this screen's own blinking; skipped with one player), 2 Speed (screen shown_speed), 3 Max. (unk_F52), 4 Ave. (lcd_race_info_average); page_step 0 writes the label, 1 updates the number. When the car reaches the goal (car at_goal) goes to lcd_race_info_finish; when the race is over (car race_over) shows the average and ends; while the LCD scrolls a text (screen lcd_scroll) waits in lcd_race_info_paused.
comment lcd_race_info_time_page Writes the Time page's label on the LCD, then the time (lcd_race_info_time).
comment lcd_race_info_time Writes the race time (the car's unk_04, in thousandths of a second) on the LCD as minutes'seconds"hundredths.
comment lcd_race_info_average_page Writes the Ave. page's label on the LCD, then the average speed (lcd_race_info_average).
comment lcd_race_info_average Writes the average speed on the LCD: the sum of the speeds (screen speed_sum) over how many there are (unk_F58), with lcd_race_info_write_number.
comment lcd_race_info_write_number Writes a number of up to three digits on the LCD, right-aligned to end before the address it is given (F1C of the screen for the km/h pages), spaces for the leading zeros.
comment lcd_race_info_finish The car has reached the goal: shows ### FINISH ### on the LCD, waits for a text to scroll across it (screen lcd_scroll) and out, shows the average speed for 150 frames (frames), then the race time, and ends.
comment lcd_race_info_paused Waits while the LCD scrolls a text (screen lcd_scroll), then goes back to lcd_race_info_update, which writes the page's label again.
comment car_radio_start Sets up the radio as the race starts: the car each player chose (byte 49 of its race_copy record) by car number in car_models (FF where none) and how many in cars_to_introduce, this screen's car number, clears screen nearing_fork, gives the car the song of its model (car song = rom_019421 by car model), notes the stage (screen route_stage), then car_radio_wait_start.
comment car_radio_wait_start Waits for the start (the car object's unk_10, the frames to it, down to 0), then goes on to car_radio_read_buttons.
comment car_radio_read_buttons Each frame of the race: the buttons newly pressed (screen buttons_pressed) into buttons, then car_radio_update. Once the race is over (car at_goal at the goal, or unk_182) clears them, silences the DJ (talk_step 5), turns the screen's two lamps off (unk_F20) and leaves car_radio_update alone, without the buttons.
comment car_radio_update The radio, by state: 0 plays the car's song (car song) with its title; 1 << and >> step through the ten songs (both together: song 10, Jingle Bell), START does >>, the DJ/music button goes to the station; 2 the station: title "Mega Radio Station", DJ message 29 queued, the stage's sound (rom_019458 by screen route_stage); 3 the station plays; 4-8 while screen nearing_fork is set (fork_camera_update sets it, a new route_stage clears it): 3F to the screen's sound byte (fade_out_music_at), sound E0 after 60 frames, sound EE, 7F when it clears and after 120 frames the stage's message (rom_019571 by screen current_stage_level) and sound. On the station: any of the three buttons goes back to the car's song (state 0, talk_step 4); the DJ talks when no message plays (screen dj_mouth_shapes), by talk_step: 0 message 0, 1-2 each car number (message 9 + number) and its car (message 1 + model), 3 message 2A, 4 the queued_message or, at random, a list of remarks (routine_lists_1966C), 5 nothing.
comment car_radio_dj_leader A DJ remark: message 11 plus the car number of the car in the lead.
comment car_radio_dj_own_car A DJ remark: message 19 plus this screen's car number.
comment car_radio_dj_remark_2a A DJ remark: message 2A (also the end of the cars' introduction).
comment car_radio_dj_remark_2b A DJ remark: message 2B.
comment car_radio_dj_remark_2d A DJ remark: message 2D.
comment car_radio_dj_remark_2f A DJ remark: message 2F.
comment car_radio_dj_remark_2c A DJ remark: message 2C.
comment car_radio_song Shows the title of song R0 (rom_01942C: 0 Magical Sound Shower, 1 Passing Breeze, 2 Splash Wave, 3 Picture the Rivers, 4 Blow Your Cool, 5 Looking for the Rainbow, 6 Speed King, 7 Adventure, 8 Sonic Control, 9 Last Wave, 10 Jingle Bell) and returns the song's sound.
comment car_radio_show_title Starts a song title (start_song_title) for the record it is given, sends the one shown (title) away and keeps the new one in title.
comment staff_credits_code_start Starts watching for the secret code during the presentation of the cars: takes the controls held (staff_credits_code_read_controls), puts code_place at the start of the code (001ADC) and goes on to staff_credits_code_watch.
comment staff_credits_code_watch Watches the controls for the code (001ADC: shift down twice, shift up twice, the wheel left, right, left, right, the brake, the gas; a wrong one starts over); when it is complete: sound 9, the presentation 1200 frames longer (screen mode_timer), and staff_credits_code_show.
comment staff_credits_code_show Shows the staff credits (the texts at 001AE8, draw_text) for 1200 frames, then fade_out_music and staff_credits_code_done.
comment staff_credits_code_done Does nothing: the code has been used; the object stays until the presentation's objects are cleared.
comment staff_credits_code_read_controls Reads the controls as bits (0 shift up, 1 shift down, 2 the wheel right, 3 the wheel left, 4 the brake, 5 the gas, each with less needed to stay on), keeps them in controls_held and returns the ones newly on (zero set when there are none).
comment transmission_label_start_automatic Sets up the label of the automatic transmission's panel (sprite set 2C1, 24 below the panel's place; the frames show NOVICE there), then transmission_label_follow.
comment transmission_label_start_manual Sets up the label of the manual transmission's panel (sprite set 2C9, its unk_40 from rom_006E78 by the car's model; the frames show 5 SPEED there for the car coin_start takes), then transmission_label_follow.
comment transmission_label_follow Keeps the label on its panel (the panel's place plus its offsets, the panel's palette) and draws it; ends when the panel ends.
comment entry_countdown_start Sets up the invitation to join, on a seat with no player while the entry time of a game runs: the screen (set_up_background, start_palette_fades), clears the LCD (clear_lcd) with the DJ's face, the music (sound F, Mega Driver) and DJ message 3B, marks the screen (unk_E2) so the car select does not start them again, starts the WAITING YOUR ENTRY banner (entry_banner), the ring of cars (entry_car_ring, in car_ring) and the traffic behind it (entry_traffic_run), and goes on to entry_countdown_count.
comment entry_countdown_count Shows the entry time left (RACE_COPY_1's entry_frames, frames): the seconds as the big number (its unk_40), the frames into the second to the ring of cars, and on the LCD a shrinking bar (rom_006464) with the seconds; when it is over clears the screen's unk_E2, fade_out_music, and goes on to entry_countdown_done.
comment entry_countdown_done Does nothing (the number is no longer drawn): the entry time is over.
comment entry_banner_start Sets up the WAITING YOUR ENTRY banner (sprite set 287) at the centre of the screen, then entry_banner_blink.
comment entry_banner_blink Draws the banner 16 frames out of every 32.
comment entry_car_ring_start Sets up the ring of cars around the entry countdown with the first car's sprite (rom_0069B8), then entry_car_ring_draw.
comment entry_car_ring_draw Draws a ring of as many copies of a car around the countdown as frames have gone in the second (copies, set by entry_countdown_count, at most 16); with a new second, the next of the eight cars (car).
comment stage_indicator_start Sets up the STAGE label at the top left of the race screen (sprite set 2E), then stage_indicator_draw.
comment stage_indicator_draw Draws the stage's name after the label (rom_00D034 by the forks taken, screen route_stage_level: PRE-STAGE, 1 to 4, FINAL STAGE) and the label itself.
comment car_select_start Starts the car select with 630 frames (21 seconds) to choose, then car_select_run.
comment car_select_run The car select, by state: 0 sets the screen up (unless screen entry_countdown_shown says the entry countdown did: clears the LCD, the title Mega Driver, sound F, DJ message 3B), the tile layer's tables from 006920, the WELCOME! letters; 1 when the screen's data is unpacked (unk_B00) starts the eight cars on the turntable and the rest, the default car (rom_0069B0 by the player's place in the race), the time display (time_digits_start); 2 30 frames; 3 the screen's unk_F04 up to 0, then the instructions (select_car_heading_show) and the car's caption (car_caption_show); 4 the wheel turns the turntable (turn, turn_delay, angle; car model is the car in front) until the gas or START confirms or time_left runs out: race model = the car, unk_48 = the car + 1, the car's sound (rom_006DEE), at least 180 frames left, the transmission choice (transmission_choice_run); 5 the wheel picks the transmission (car transmission = rom_006DA4 by the wheel: 0 automatic, 1 manual) until confirmed or out of time; 6-8 when the entry time is over (entry_over) marks the player ready (set_player_ready) and waits for the race's other players (players_not_ready), DJ message 3C; 9 60 frames, then fade_out_music and the game's next step (screen game_mode byte 2); 10 stays. Every frame from 4 on: car_select_turn_to_choice and car_select_update_rowscroll; time_left down by one and shown in seconds.
comment car_select_turn_to_choice Once the car is chosen (car_chosen), turns the turntable the short way round until the chosen car (car model) is in front, 20 a frame, skewing the floor as it goes.
comment car_select_update_rowscroll Writes the line scroll of the tile layer for the next frames: the top 104 lines all at top_scroll, which moves one more each frame, and while rowscroll_copies says, the floor's 120 lines from their offsets at 3800 of the screen.
comment car_select_skew_floor_next Skews the turntable's floor for a step toward the next car (car_select_skew_floor with 6).
comment car_select_skew_floor_previous Skews the turntable's floor for a step toward the previous car (car_select_skew_floor with -6).
comment car_select_skew_floor Adds a step that grows line by line (by R0) to the floor's 120 line offsets at 3800 of the screen, writes them to the next two pages of the line scroll and has car_select_update_rowscroll copy them to the others (rowscroll_copies).
comment car_select_wait_confirm Whether the player has not confirmed yet (zero set): confirming is pressing the gas pedal down (screen gas from C0, after letting it up under 40) or START, once confirm_delay has run out.
comment welcome_letter_start Starts the eight letters of WELCOME! (itself the first), then welcome_letter_show.
comment welcome_letter_show Shows its letter of WELCOME! (font 346): appears 2 frames after the one before it, cycles its colours (step_palette, from 006DCC) and ends 45 frames after the start.
comment clear_lcd Clears the cabinet's LCD: sixteen spaces, no DJ message (screen dj_mouth_shapes), no scrolling text (unk_F40, unk_F44), the DJ's face off (unk_F35).
comment start_dj_message Starts DJ message R0 (typed_messages): requests its voice (the sound in the record's top byte) and has the face on the LCD move its mouth by the record's string (screen dj_mouth_shapes).
comment start_dj_message_if_quiet Starts DJ message R0 (start_dj_message) unless one is being said (screen dj_mouth_shapes).
comment start_song_title Starts an object that shows the song title of record R10 (x, then the text) on the screen and scrolls it on the LCD; returns the object.
comment random_number Returns the next pseudo-random number of the screen's sequence (kept in unk_F00).
comment request_sound Requests sound R0 for the screen (queue_sound), except while the screen's unk_90 bit 8 is set, when only the sounds the table at 01202E allows play, and only with advertise_sound set.
comment queue_sound Puts sound R0 in the screen's ring of 32 sound requests (801 of the screen: the place, then the requests).
comment draw_text Draws the text record R19 (x and y, an attribute, a string closed by 0) as one sprite per character and returns the address of the record after it.
comment step_palette Gives the object the next palette (palette, an index into the sprites' palette words, rom_032BA4) of its palette_list; a negative entry goes back by that many bytes, to loop.
comment binary_to_bcd Converts R0 into eight decimal digits, one per four bits, the units in the lowest.
comment set_player_ready Marks the screen's player ready in its race record (unk_5C), after its selections.
comment players_not_ready Whether a player of the race has not marked itself ready yet (zero set): byte 5C of each one's race_copy record.

01478B  func  goal_actor_rider_place
014838  func  goal_actor_start
014846  func  goal_actor_run
017BFB  func  palette_cycle_update
017CAA  func  sunset_fade_start
017D1F  func  sunset_fade_wait
017E1B  func  backdrop_near_start
017E5B  func  backdrop_near_update
017EF2  func  backdrop_landmark_start
017F3D  func  backdrop_landmark_update
0180CB  func  backdrop_translucent_start
018110  func  backdrop_translucent_update
01818A  func  backdrop_off_screen
comment road_object_stream_read Starts the objects along the road of the stage being built (road_object_streams). By state: 0 takes the stream of the stage being built (screen built_stage); 1 reads the stream's records while their position is not beyond the segments the road generator has built of the stage (car stage_segments): command 0 road_object_stream_start_object, 1 road_object_stream_stage_end, 2 road_object_stream_end, 3 road_object_stream_fork; 2 (the stage's stream is over) waits until the generator builds the stage the route goes to (screen built_stage = stage), then stage_start = car built_segments (the segment the new stage starts at) and its stream; 3 (a fork) waits until the route's next stage is known (screen route_stage no longer stage), then goes on with the stream of the branch the car takes (car branch) from the fork's table, the same stage_start.
comment road_object_stream_start_object Command 0 of a stream: starts an object (start_object) at the record's position plus stage_start (field 1C), with the record's handler and its parameter in field 18 (most often the address of the object's record in the ROM), and moves the stream past the record.
comment road_object_stream_stage_end Command 1: the stage's stream is over; notes the stage the route goes to next (screen route_stage) in stage and waits for it (state 2).
comment road_object_stream_fork Command 3, a fork: keeps the address of the table of the branches' streams in stream, the current stage (screen built_stage) in stage, and waits for the branch (state 3).
comment road_object_stream_end Command 2: the last stage's stream is over (the goals): ends the reader.
comment crest_clip_update Cuts the sprites beyond the road's crests: each frame, for the crests the road's projection found (screen crest_count of them; their depth layers at 1F10 of the screen and their screen heights at 1F30), each whose layer is lower than all those before it gets a sprite clip command in its layer (draw_mode 6: the 16-byte entry at clip_command, command 7000: the drawing area from the top of the screen down to clip_bottom = the crest's height + screen sprite_origin_y - 1, across clip_left..clip_right), so the sprites the list draws after it (meant: what lies beyond the crest) are cut at the crest's height.
comment traffic_spawner_update Starts the traffic of the stage the race's leader is in (object_lists_2E1F0). By state: 0 the list of the stage being built (screen built_stage), traffic_level = 8 - the number of players (byte 1F of the record screen host_record points at) + the game setting game_difficulty, the draw distance into screen traffic_draw_distance, no traffic yet (screen traffic_count); 1 (outside the attract demo, screen demo_flags bit 8) when the leader's stage (screen leader_stage) changes, takes its list with positions from where the leader was then (screen leader_window_segment); starts each record (position, level, handler, stop_distance, record) whose position is within the draw distance of the leader's view (unk_46 of the race record screen leader_record), when its level is not above traffic_level and fewer than 32 are on the road (screen traffic_count); then writes at F84 of the screen the stretches of road the players can see (each player's view, unk_46 of the race records listed after byte 1F, give or take the draw distance; a count, then each stretch's top and bottom from the highest), for traffic_out_of_view.
comment traffic_car_start Sets up a traffic car from its record (the address of its sprite record, then side, x_offset and speed): from the sprite record its animation (animation_start), palette, passing_sound, hit_sound, weight and hit_box (the sprite record's last 8 bytes); draw_mode B (the frame chosen by the angle it is seen from, traffic_car_draw), scale_shift FF, passed 0; goes on to state 1.
comment traffic_car_draw Moves and draws a traffic car: keeps the position_fraction; ends it (taking it off the count, screen traffic_count) when traffic_out_of_view; plays passing_sound whenever the screen's car (race_copy segment) passes it or it passes the car (passed); while it is within the draw distance ahead of the view (race_copy window_segment) picks its frame by the angle the player sees it from: a column (4-1B) from the difference between the road's direction (+0 of the road's segment record) at the car and at it, plus a quarter of its screen_x, and a row (0-3, 20 frames apart) from the difference in height (+2) and the car's pitch (car view_pitch), shaken for 20 frames after a hit (shake, rom_013D45); traffic_hit_check, and on a hit traffic_start_hit_sound and shake = 1; draw_object.
comment traffic_start_hit_sound When the traffic object has a hit_sound, starts an object (013D15) that plays it 15 frames later.
comment traffic_out_of_view Whether road position R0 is outside every stretch of road the race's players see (zero set: the traffic there is removed): the stretches traffic_spawner writes at F84 of the screen (a count, then each stretch's top and bottom).
comment traffic_hit_check Whether the screen's car hits this traffic object (negative set), unless the car's object has flags bit 3 set: the car's box (the entry SCREEN(screen)->unk_500 points at) against the object's hit_box (along the road from (position - the car's first segment) * 128 + position_fraction, across from the road's width * side + x_offset). On a hit the car's object gets flags bit 2, byte 6E = 1 (the kind of hit: a single object) and 68 = this object, whose speed and weight the crash (007DBD) reads.
comment traffic_car_update A traffic car, drawn from the angle the player sees it at. By state: 0 traffic_car_start; 1 moves along the road by speed (128ths of a segment a frame, position_fraction carrying into position), then traffic_car_draw; once the screen's car has reached the goal (car at_goal) it ends when it is 8 segments behind the view.
comment traffic_car_start_convoy The first vehicle of a convoy (the only one: stage 25, animation 35C followed by eight 35D): its record starts with the distance between them and the records of those that follow, closed by 0; starts each (handler 013AB7: traffic_car_update, kept live as long as the one before it is, which ends it) that distance behind the one before, keeping the one before in its field 14, with the same stop_distance, counted in screen traffic_count; then becomes a traffic_car with the record after the list.
comment traffic_stopping_car_update A traffic car that slows to a stop: as traffic_car_update, but at each segment it counts stop_distance down and, once it has run out (stopping), its speed drops by one a segment to 0. The cars of stages 2 and 12, and what the traffic_figure teams pull (013AD8 runs it).
comment traffic_figure_start_team The leader of a team (the horses pulling a covered wagon in stage 1, the reindeer pulling a sleigh in stages 25 and 26): its record starts with the distance and the records of what it pulls, closed by 0; starts each (handler 013AD8: traffic_stopping_car_update, kept live as long as the one before it is, which ends it) that distance behind the one before (its field 14 the one before, the same stop_distance, counted in screen traffic_count), then becomes a traffic_figure with the record after the list.
comment traffic_figure_update Traffic drawn from a single view (draw_mode 5) instead of by angle: the horses of the covered wagons (stage 1), the reindeer (stages 25, 26) and the figures of stages 10 and 24 (animation 185) and 30 (258, 071). By state: 0 reads its record as traffic_car_start does (draw_mode 4, scale_shift 2 and y_offset -2C0 for animation 165; scale_shift 0 for 258 and 185, else 1); 1 once the screen's car has reached the goal (car at_goal) ends when 8 segments behind the view; moves along the road by speed, slowing to a stop once stop_distance has run out (stopping); ends when traffic_out_of_view; while within the draw distance ahead of the view: traffic_hit_check when it has a hit box (traffic_start_hit_sound on a hit), draw_object and animation_advance.
comment stage_entry_update The start of a stage's road (position 0 of streams 1-30): when the car (car segment) reaches it, the stage the car is in becomes the one whose road is being built (screen current_stage_level = unk_A2, its level; unk_A6 = unk_A3, its number; car reached_stage_start = unk_48, the segment it starts at), and it ends. In the attract demo (screen demo_flags bit 8) it ends at once.
comment aurora_update The northern lights over stage 26 (stream 26 at 1040: two of them, with their own lists): drawn at a screen position (draw_mode 0) that slides in from x -160 to 160, 8 a frame (y 40, or 30 for car model 6, car model), then stays; it steps through its list of animations (aurora_next_animation, each shown animation_frames frames, aurora_count_down) and is drawn only every other frame (when countdown is odd), so it looks faint. It never ends.
comment aurora_count_down Counts the current animation's frames down; when they run out, aurora_next_animation.
comment aurora_next_animation Starts the next animation of the list (animation_list; a negative entry goes back that many bytes first) for animation_frames frames.
comment kangaroo_update The kangaroos of stage 21 (Australia) that hop across the road ahead of the car. By state: 0 reads the record (animation, hop_animation, palette, flags, side, x_offset, trigger_distance, x_speed, draw_mode, scale_shift), trigger_position = position - trigger_distance, appear_position = position - draw_distance; 1 not drawn until the view (race_copy window_segment) passes appear_position; 2 drawn standing until the car (car segment) reaches trigger_position, then hop_animation; 3 while the car (car window_segment) has not reached it: x_offset += x_speed each frame and it moves along the road at the car's own pace (position_fraction += the car's speed, car speed, >> 9), animation_advance and draw_object; it ends when the car reaches it, when it gets beyond draw_distance or when its screen_x leaves -F0..130.
comment swooping_helicopter_update The helicopter over the waterfalls of stage 4: it swoops down, then flies off. By state: 0 reads the record (animation, palette, flags, draw_mode, scale_shift, side, x_offset, y_offset, trigger_distance + 16, x_speed, y_speed, road_speed, dive_frames, dive_y_speed), trigger_position = position - trigger_distance, appear_position = position - draw_distance; 1 not drawn until the view (race_copy window_segment) passes trigger_position, then dive_countdown = dive_frames; 2 for dive_frames frames y_offset += dive_y_speed and position += road_speed; 3 x_offset += x_speed, y_offset += y_speed, position += road_speed; from 2 on, animation_advance and draw_object while within draw_distance of the car (car window_segment); it ends when the car reaches it, when it gets beyond draw_distance or when its screen_x leaves -F0..130.
comment start_gate_update The START banner over the start line and its two posts with the signal lights (stream 0 at 20: four of them). By state: 0 reads the record as road_row_start does (animation, palette, flags, draw_mode, scale_shift, side, x_offset, y_offset, spacing, count, hit box; first_position, end_position), and goes on to state 2 without a hit box; 1 road_row_crash_check and draw_object (no record has a hit box); 2 the lights: palette from rom_01306E by the seconds to the start (the car object's unk_10, in frames, rounded up): C9 until the last 3 seconds, then CC, CB, and CA at the start, which goes on to state 3; 3 draw_object. It never ends.
comment start_flagman_update The man at the start line who waves the chequered flag as the race starts (stream 0 at 20). By state: 0 reads the record (animation, go_animation, palette, side, x_offset, y_offset), draw_mode 9, scale_shift 1; 1 drawn standing (06E) while the car object's unk_10 counts the frames to the start, then go_animation (06F, the waving); 1 and 2: ends once the car (car window_segment) reaches its position, else animation_advance and draw_object.
comment start_balloon_update The balloons let go over the start line as the race starts (stream 0 at 24-34). By state: 0 reads the record (animation, palette, side, x_offset, y_offset), draw_mode 9, scale_shift 1; 1 not drawn until the start (the car object's unk_10 down to 0); 1 and 2: it rises faster and faster (rise_speed + 1 each frame, y_offset - rise_speed / 2) and drifts (x_offset + 8), animation_advance and draw_object; it ends when the car (car window_segment) reaches its position or y_offset passes -3000 (high above).
comment ambient_sound_update A sound repeated at random along a stretch of road (the jungle of stage 3: sound 51): plays it, then again after 15 to 78 frames at random (random_number), until the car (car segment) has gone length segments from where it started.
comment one_shot_sound_update Plays sound 23 once and ends: placed at the start of stage 30's stream, so it sounds as the road of stage 30 starts being built.
comment west_east_panel_update The WEST BOUND / EAST BOUND panel at the bottom centre of the screen before the first fork (stream 0 at 550): animation 39 at a screen position (draw_mode A, layer 19D), its palette cycling through base_palette to base_palette + 3 (a step every 4 frames, colour_count). By state: 0 sets it up below the screen (y 118); 1 rises 2 a frame to y DC; 1 and 2: once the car (car segment) passes 250 or the race time runs out (word 6A of the race record screen host_record points at), 3: goes down 2 a frame and ends at y 118.
comment santa_sleigh_update Santa's sleigh and reindeer flying back and forth over the goal of stage 26, with sound 49 (sound) every 15 frames. By state: 0 reads the record (animation, palette, flags, side, x_offset, x_speed, sound, draw_mode, scale_shift; y_offset -100 for animation 3A0, -180 for 3A2), appear_position = position - draw_distance (and 58 = position - 100, never read); 1 not drawn until the view (race_copy window_segment) passes appear_position; 2 moves by x_speed (leftward) until its screen_x is below -B0, then 3: mirrored (flags bit 6), moves back until screen_x is above F0, then 2 again; each frame animation_advance and draw_object. It never ends (the race ends at the goal).
comment goal_parade_rightward_update What runs back and forth in front of a goal's crowd, setting off rightward: a kangaroo (stage 21), a dragon dance (22), an orca leaping over the sea (28). By state: 0 reads the record (animation, palette, flags, side, x_offset, x_speed, draw_mode, scale_shift; y_offset -180 for animation 3A2), appear_position = position - draw_distance (and 58 = position - 100, never read); 1 not drawn until the view (race_copy window_segment) passes appear_position; 2 moves by x_speed (mirrored by the record's flags bit 6) until its screen_x passes B0, then 3: unmirrored, moves back until screen_x is below -F0, then 2 again; each frame animation_advance and draw_object. It never ends.
comment goal_parade_leftward_update What runs back and forth in front of a goal's crowd, setting off leftward: a kangaroo (stage 21), an orca (28), the bulls (29). As goal_parade_rightward_update, with the turns the other way: 2 moves by x_speed (negative) until its screen_x is below -B0, then 3: mirrored (flags bit 6), moves back until screen_x is above F0, then 2 again. It never ends.
comment goal_parade_dragon_update The dragon dance in front of the crowd at the goal of stage 22 (with goal_parade_rightward's second dragon): as goal_parade_leftward_update, plus sound 44 on one frame in four at random (random_number). It never ends.
comment sounding_scenery_update Animated scenery at a goal that makes a sound every second: the pandas at Beijing (stage 24, sound 47), the cheering group with banners in Japan (23, sound 4A), at the Matterhorn (27, sound 46). By state: 0 reads its record as road_row_start does (animation, palette, flags, size_table, scale_shift, side, x_offset, y_offset, spacing, count), with a sound where a hit box would be; 1 and 2: every 60 frames (sound_countdown) the sound, animation_advance and road_row_draw. (State 3, the same without the sound, is never reached.)
comment fork_branch_start_right Starts the other branch of the road off to the right (placed on the left branch's stream: the car took the left way): direction -1, then fork_branch_draw.
comment fork_branch_start_left Starts the other branch of the road off to the left (on the right branch's stream): direction 1, then fork_branch_draw.
comment fork_branch_draw Draws the branch of the road the car did not take, after a fork, as a strip of sprites curving away to one side (direction) along the 40 segments from position: for each segment in view (the road's per-segment records from the view, screen + 3000) the piece of road (fork_branch_sprite) goes at the road's screen x plus a curve growing with the distance from start_position (rom_101400) times the road's scale times direction, the pieces between segments filled in, into the screen's sprite list and its segment's depth list, until the strip leaves the screen. Ends once the view (race_copy window_segment) passes end_position.
comment fork_branch_sprite Builds the sprite words of the branch's road piece for fork_branch_draw: the first frame of animation 74 (its size and place in the sprite ROM, the size steps at 0x122B74) and the palette of the stage being built (rom_0140C8 by screen built_stage, into rom_032BA4).
comment stage_end_sounds_update The sounds as the car nears the end of a stage (before each fork's checkpoint and each goal): sound 24, then sound 53 20 frames later (the list at 01466F: a delay, then sounds and delays closed by 0); when the race record's stage count (race_copy stage_count) changes (the checkpoint or the goal passed), sound 24 if this screen's player leads the race (screen player = unk_70), else 28, and it ends.
comment fork_camera_update Raises and tilts the view before each fork (256 segments before it), so the fork is seen from above. By state: 0 waits until the car (race_copy segment) reaches its position; 1 each frame the view's pitch lift (car view_pitch_lift) up by lift_step to lift_height (1E0 less the car model's view_pitch_base, rom_010BD2 by race_copy model; screen nearing_fork = FF when it gets there: the radio's checkpoint message) and its shift lift (car view_shift_lift) down by tilt_step; 2 holds until the car reaches lower_position (224 segments on); 3 lowers it back the same way and ends. Does nothing while car crash_body_shown is set.
comment goal_actor_start Sets an actor of the goal scene to a screen position (draw_mode 0) and goes on to goal_actor_run.
comment goal_actor_run Plays the actor's script (script; step is where this frame's step began), a step a frame: a halfword of flags, from bit 15: wait (counter to the next halfword, drawn), repeat from step (until the counter passes the next halfword), animation_advance, frame =, x +=, x =, y +=, y =, scale += and = (34, 36), layer += and =, a new animation, palette =, a sound (request_sound); with bit 0 clear the step ends and the actor is drawn (draw_object); with bit 0 set a command (rom_014994): 0 tells the scene object it is over (its state = 2), 1 ends the actor, 2 ends the step undrawn, 3 flips it (flags bit 6), 4 car segment_progress + 4000, 5 a repeat whose count grows by 8 for each place behind (rom_014A16 by screen + 320 + unk_01).
comment goal_actor_car_start The player's car in the goal scene: its animation, palette and frame from the race record (race_copy animation, unk_3E, unk_4A), its layer, place and scale from the scene object (scene: the car's drawing object, 00974C's, whose handler is 014694 at the goal); then goal_actor_start.
comment goal_actor_driver_start The driver in the goal scene: goal_actor_rider_place with the scene object's first rider record (+90).
comment goal_actor_passenger_start The passenger in the goal scene: goal_actor_rider_place with the scene object's second rider record (+A0).
comment goal_actor_rider_place Places a rider in the scene's car: from the car's current frame (the car's animation and frame from race_copy animation, unk_4A) the view it is drawn from (bit 6 of the frame's attributes picks the mirrored set) and the seat's offset; from the rider record (R12: a table of offsets by view, the animation of the first view, the palette, the frame) the rider's animation, palette and frame; scaled by the scene object's scale onto its place, in its layer; then goal_actor_start.
comment palette_cycle_update Cycles palettes of the stage's scenery (object_lists_181B8): after delay frames, every step_frames frames queues the next palette set of its list (palette_list; a negative entry goes back that many bytes) with queue_palette_list, for frames_left frames (or for good when it is 0); it waits while the stage's background is changing (screen background_fading) and ends when it changes again (running).
comment sunset_fade_start Fades the colours of the stage toward evening after a while (object_lists_181B8: Hawaii's sunset after 900 frames, stage 26's dusk after 300 and its night after 600): waits count frames (not while the background changes, screen background_fading), then starts a palette fade (start_palette_fade) for each entry of its list (fade_list: from, to, the palette, the speed), keeps them in fades and their number in count, and goes on to sunset_fade_wait.
comment sunset_fade_wait Waits for its palette fades to finish, then ends; if the stage's background starts changing (screen background_fading) it stops them and ends.
comment backdrop_near_start Sets up a sprite on the background a little nearer than the panorama (the hill of the Acropolis with the Parthenon, stages 14 and 19): from its data the animation, palette, panorama_x, height and half_width; draw_mode A, layer 1; straight to state 1 when the background is being set up (screen background_at_start); then backdrop_near_update.
comment backdrop_near_update Keeps the sprite on the background, moving with its panorama a quarter faster (nearer): x from the car's heading (car view_yaw, a quarter of it until screen background_changing is set) less panorama_x, y from the background's height (screen background_scroll_y) and height, but not below screen road_top_line's line. By state: 0 waits until it is off the screen (so it does not pop up in view); 1 draw_object while on the screen; off it, it ends once leaving.
comment backdrop_landmark_start Sets up a landmark on the horizon that grows as the car approaches (Tiananmen in stage 10, the castle of stages 16 and 17...): as backdrop_near_start, layer 2, and approach_start = the car's segment (car window_segment) less the distance in its data; then backdrop_landmark_update.
comment backdrop_landmark_update Keeps the landmark on the background's panorama (as backdrop_near_update, a quarter faster than the panorama) and grows it with the distance travelled: its scale (width_scale, height_scale) follows the sine of (car window_segment - approach_start) / 2 (rom_100C00) up to full size (100, state 2). By state: 0 waits until it is off the screen; 1 and 2 drawn while on the screen; off it, it ends once leaving.
comment backdrop_translucent_start Sets up a sprite on the background that is drawn every other frame, so it looks faint (the pyramids of stages 13 and 20, the spray of stage 4's falls): as backdrop_near_start, layer 1, frame_count 0; then backdrop_translucent_update.
comment backdrop_translucent_update Keeps the sprite on the background's panorama at the panorama's own speed (as backdrop_far_update), animates it and draws it only on even frames (frame_count), so it looks faint. By state: 0 waits until it is off the screen; 1 drawn while on the screen; off it, it ends once leaving.
comment backdrop_off_screen Whether the background sprite is wholly off the screen (zero set; it clears flags bit 5 then): its x (field 30) more than half_width beyond the screen's edges (0..140).

# ======== the player's car and the race display (naming batch D) ========
006EF0  func  player_car_update

01256F  func  player_car_check_car_hits
