comment player_car_update The screen's car, each frame: player_car_check_place, player_car_read_controls, player_car_build_hit_boxes, then by state: 0 sets up the race (the view's constants in screen eye_distance-18, the course, the car on its grid place: segment 16 + rom_0091CC by players and place, road_x from rom_0090AC, its world position; the palette of its model, rom_009510; timer 8 frames in the demo, 149 in a race with the intro view car view_shift_intro/unk_10E; the gears, torque curves, crash physics, with screen is_host the race's time left (race time_left: 40 seconds plus master_time_difficulty), the engine sound); 1 the countdown to the start: brake full and the wheel centred until timer runs out (others read it as the frames to the start), then car moving and race started; 2 driving (player_car_move): goes to 3 when the time is up (car time_up), to 4 at the goal (car at_goal); counts hit_guard down; on a hit (flags bit 2, by hit_kind): 3 a fork sign slows it for 15 frames (car slowed_frames), 0 scenery, 1 a road object (hit_other), 2 the other player's car (hit_other): a speed change, a push and a bounce from the model's crash table (car crash_table, by impact_speed or the cars' speeds and places), the hit's sounds, a crash animation, the CRASH text (car crash_text), immune (flags bit 3), state 5; 5 the crash plays out: no controls, the push and the speed change die away, it bounces under gravity (sound 31 on landing), back to 2 when the animation ends with 15 frames of hit_guard; 3 time up: gas off and the model's brake (rom_009530), 180 frames, then the race is over (car race_over), GAME OVER (004771) with one player or race_result_start with two, 60 frames, screen flags bit 4 and DJ message 36 or 37; 4 the goal: finished, the race's stage count up, waits for the screen's finish time (screen 410) into race finish_time and the race time, race finished, then drives itself (player_car_steer_to_stop) to its stop_position by its place (rom_009540), plays the model's arrival animation (rom_009520), brakes to a stop, 90 frames, screen flags bit 4.
comment player_car_move One frame of the car on the road: player_car_drive, player_car_place_view, player_car_move_view, player_car_set_horizon, player_car_publish, player_car_extend_course, player_car_project_road, player_car_engine_sound and player_car_animate; no wheel effects while it crashes (state 5).
comment player_car_steer_to_stop Drives the car by itself after the goal: gas 40, no brake, the speed (car speed) a 16th of the way to stop_speed each frame, and the wheel turned step by step toward stop_x across the road (centred within 8 of it; stop_wheel keeps it).
comment player_car_check_place Keeps the race order around the screen's car: screen leader_record-6C the race records (unk_70-73 the players) of the leader, the last, the car just ahead and the car just behind (all itself in the demo), screen leader_window_segment and unk_352 the leader's race window_segment and unk_62. When its place (screen 320 + player, kept in place) changes during the race: sound 37 when the car it passed or that passed it is within 160 across, else 3A. While first, blinks one of the screen's lamps (the count, period and timer bytes C9-CB of its entry at checkpoint_lamp).
comment player_car_publish Hands the car's state on: this frame's view into the screen's ring of eight (screen + 600, slot unk_D4, also race view_slot) and the view of the slot race_copy view_slot names back into the car and screen shown_view, so that both screens draw the view of the same frame; and the car's world position, animation, palette, frame, wheel effects, speed, sideways speed, flags, segments, place across the road and bounce into its race record (race published set), from which race_car draws it on both screens. Not in ISDN mode (isdn_mode), which reads them back instead.
comment player_car_set_horizon Works out the horizon's line on the screen (screen horizon_line) from the view's tilt: the nod (car view_pitch_nod), the rival's and the fork camera's pulls (view_pitch_rival, view_pitch_lift), the car model's base pitch (screen view_pitch_base), clamped to C0-200, and the intro's (view_pitch_intro).
comment player_car_place_on_road Puts the car on its starting segment at the start: no way into the segment, road_x from the grid's place, its world position (player_car_world_position) and the view behind it (player_car_place_view).
comment player_car_clear_hit Ends what a hit was doing to the car: no sideways_push, speed_change, bounce, no hit pending (flags bit 2), and the car no longer skidding (car skidding) or slipping (unk_65).
comment player_car_animate Picks the car's animation: while a hit is pending, the one it has goes on; skidding (car skidding), a skid by how far it slides (slide) toward the side the wheel is turned (rom_009310, rom_009410 by model); spinning its wheels while it speeds up (car wheel_slip, acceleration above 0), the model's wheelspin (rom_009300); else its body turned by the steering (player_car_steer_frame). Each one in the row of the view's height (player_car_view_height); a new one starts at frame 0.
comment player_car_steer_frame The car's body as it steers: the model's animation (rom_00926C) in the row of the view's height, its frame (steer_frame) one step a frame toward the one of the steering (the bytes around 0092BC: 0-8, 4 straight on).
comment player_car_view_height The row of the car's animations for how steeply the view looks down on it: the view's pitch (car view_pitch) less B0 and less the road's pitch under the car (race_copy segment_pitch), in steps of 30, 0 to 5 (view_height).
comment player_car_move_view Moves the view with what the car does: when a rival comes close (player_car_check_rivals, rival_step) it starts the RIVAL SEKKIN warning (rival_warning) and pulls the view back and up (car view_shift_rival to -256, unk_112 +8 a step) until the rival has gone, then lets it back; a speed gain of more than A0 in a frame without wheelspin sets car hard_acceleration (hard acceleration); the view nods (nod, car view_shift_nod and unk_102) one way with that hard acceleration and the other with hard braking (more than 120 a frame with the brake down), easing back; and it lags the car's rises and drops (car view_rise).
comment player_car_check_rivals Whether a rival is close (rival_near): at speed 6000 or more (5000 to stay on), the car just ahead (screen ahead_record) a little slower and within reach, or the car just behind (unk_6C) a little faster and close; once on it stays for 30 frames (rival_hold) after the rival has gone.
comment player_car_load_crash_physics Loads the crash physics of the car's model (rom_00804A): bounce_damping, speed_change_decay, push_decay, gravity and the address of its crash table.
comment player_car_load_gears Loads the car's model and transmission: its handling record (car handling), its torque curves (unk_50), its gear ratios (unk_54) and top gear (unk_63), the first gear's ratio (unk_64), and the roadside surfaces of the first stage (player_car_next_surfaces).
comment player_car_build_torque_curves Builds the drive force of each gear by speed at screen + 1300 (256 values a gear), interpolating the model's points (car torque_curves).
comment player_car_read_controls Takes the controls: gas, brake and wheel from the screen's pedals and wheel (screen gas, unk_102, unk_100). In the demo (screen demo_flags bit 8) the car drives itself: no brake, the gas up to F0 (eased off while skidding), the wheel back toward the middle when off the road or slowed, else against its sideways drift.
comment player_car_drive Moves the car for the frame: the drag (player_car_road_drag), the slope of its segment (race segment_pitch), the brake (no gas while braking; brake lights car brake_lights) and the gas through the gear's torque curve go to player_car_engine (speed, revs); the wheel steers through the model's table (less at low speed) into steering; the road's turn under it (previous_heading less heading) pushes it out (less with catch-up, car catch_up); beyond the model's grip it skids (car skidding, slide, tyre noise 3, smoke on both sides); sideways_speed and sideways_push move it across (car road_x, within the road's half width + 60); the speed along (less its sideways angle, vector_angle) plus a catch-up bonus by the distance to the car ahead (car catch_up_target, unk_6A; not once finished) moves it along, passing segments (car window_segment, unk_44, unk_40, unk_A4, unk_34, unk_32); then player_car_world_position. In ISDN mode (isdn_mode) the race record the link brings in gives all this.
comment player_car_world_position Places the car in the world from its segment: world_x and world_z from the segment's position and the car's place across and along it turned by the road's heading and pitch (rotate_offset), world_y the road's height there plus bounce_height (the old one to previous_world_y); the rest of the segment (car segment_remaining) and the road's heading under the car (heading, the old one to previous_heading).
comment player_car_place_view Places the view behind the car: a point on the road some way back of the car (the nod, the intro, the rival and road-object pulls: car view_shift_nod, unk_10C, unk_110, unk_108) and across (unk_106) and above (unk_104) it (view_target_x/y/z), looking toward the road twelve segments on (vector_angles: car view_yaw the yaw, unk_30 the road's pitch; car view_pitch the pitch with the view's tilt), the eye screen eye_distance back from the target along those angles (angles_to_offset) in car view_x-28.
comment player_car_engine The engine and the speed: the force (brake, slope and drag, drive; acceleration) changes the speed (car speed, also impact_speed), with any speed_change from a hit, up to the top speed of the gear (its ratio, car gear_ratio); a force past the model's grip spins the wheels, and hard braking locks them (car wheel_slip: tyre noise 1 or the model's skid noise, smoke); the revs (car revs) follow the speed through the gear's ratio, or the gas when the car stands; car moving says it moves.
comment player_car_next_surfaces Takes the roadside surfaces of the stage (screen built_stage): the two lists of rom_00B78C for it (car surface_entry_1, unk_14C), from the segment where the stage starts (car surface_start) to the next one's (unk_4C).
comment player_car_road_drag The drag on the car: speed squared, times the model's off-road factor while slowed (car slowed_frames) or off the road (car off_road, with the off-road shake); and the roadside surfaces under its wheels (car surface_entry_1, unk_14C) give the effects at each side (car wheel_effect_sides: bit 0 with unk_141, bit 1 with unk_142).
comment player_car_off_road_shake Off the road: the model's shake to the screen's bits of output port D (wheel_motor, the wheel motor's; rom_00B704) and its off-road noise (rom_00B6FC).
comment player_car_start_course Starts the race's course (rom_03CC00 by screen first_stage, car course): clears the ring of segments, the first road width, and builds the segments as far as the view reaches (screen draw_distance >> 7, prebuilt_segments); the first stage into the stage history.
comment player_car_extend_course Keeps the course built 104 segments past the window's start (window_record, build_end_offset). At a fork the building waits (at_fork) until the checkpoint is passed (screen forks_decided): then the branch taken (screen 349 + the fork) goes on: branch, the course of that branch (car course), its stage (screen route_stage, stage indicator A5), the stage and branch histories and forks_taken, the stage's data queued (queue_stage_load) and the branch_arrow.
comment player_car_build_segment Builds the next segment of the road from the course (car course): at the end of a run reads the next records: 0 the course ends (the car's state 3), 1 a jump to another part, 2 a fork (forks_reached, the race's stage count, at_fork), 3 and 5 the pitch and the heading back to 0, 4 a new stage (screen built_stage_level, unk_A3; stage_segments, stage_start), 6 the goal (a goal object at this segment), else a run (its length, pitch and heading steps, road width, a byte for the segments). The segment: heading and pitch turned by the run's steps, the position moved along them (next_x/y/z), the width eased toward the run's; written at build_offset, then built_segments and stage_segments count up.
comment player_car_project_road Projects the road for the view of screen shown_view (player_car_publish): its rotation (view_matrix_*, from the view's pitch and yaw), then each segment from the window's start out to the view's distance turned into the view and scaled by its depth into the screen's list at screen + 3000 (scale, width, x, y, the segment's byte), segments behind the view or too far left out.
comment player_car_start_engine_sound Starts the car's engine sound on the screen's channel of the sound board: the model's engine sample (rom_012026) and the engine's loudness by the transmission (10, or 30, 6 dB louder, with the transmission's value set), then the revs on (1) and the requests for the sample and the pitch (A0); silent in the demo without sound.
comment player_car_engine_sound Sets the engine's pitch from the revs (car revs) each frame, or silences it in the demo without sound.
comment player_car_build_hit_boxes Builds the hit boxes of the race's cars at screen + 510: how many, then for each its player, race record and box (player_car_write_hit_box from its segment, model, place along and across; screen own_hit_box the screen's own), and the screen's car's box from its own fields at screen + 5B0.
comment player_car_write_hit_box Writes a car's hit box: its record, its first and last segment (4 on), and its box along and across from the model's size (rom_0126BC).
comment player_car_check_car_hits Whether the car hits another car of the race (the boxes of player_car_build_hit_boxes; not while either is immune, flags bit 3): a hit of kind 2 with that car's player in hit_other.
comment vector_angle Returns the 12-bit angle of the vector (R0 - R2, R1 - R3). Used by player_car_drive.
comment vector_angles Returns the angles from a point (R3, R4, R5: x, y, z) toward another (R0, R1, R2): the pitch in R0, the yaw in R1. Used by player_car_place_view.
comment rotate_offset Turns an offset (R1 across, R2 along, R0 up) by a heading (R3) and a pitch (R4) into the world's axes. Used by player_car_world_position and player_car_place_view.
comment angles_to_offset Returns the offset of length R2 along pitch R0 and yaw R1 (R3, R4, R5: x, y, z). Used by player_car_place_view for the eye behind the view's target.
comment set_tyre_noise Sets the noise of the screen's car's tyres on the sound board (byte 718 of the screen's page; the sound program keeps it while the game keeps writing it and keys it off 255 of its main loop's passes after the last): wheelspin, skids, off-road rumble. In the demo without sound it mutes the engine instead. Used by player_car (player_car_engine, player_car_off_road_shake, the goal) and 00664E.
comment set_tyre_noise_on_road set_tyre_noise, unless the car is off the road (car off_road), where the off-road noise plays. Used by player_car_drive, player_car_engine and gear_indicator_update.
comment queue_stage_load Queues the palette of the stage the screen goes to (rom_031DDC by screen route_stage) through queue_palette (the palette queue at screen + 900). Used by player_car_extend_course and the game's code (002E74).
comment rev_meter_update The rev meter, the bar left of the speed at the bottom right: state 0 sets it up (animation 6, at F2, D6), 1 draws the frame of the revs (car revs >> 11, 32 steps).
comment speedometer_update The speed in km/h at the bottom right: state 0 sets it up (digits of animation 12, units_x, digits_y) and clears the screen's maximum (unk_F52), sum (unk_F54) and count (unk_F58); 1 each frame the car's speed / 88 into screen shown_speed, and while moving adds it to the sum, counts it and keeps the maximum (for the LCD's pages), then draws its digits from the units leftwards.
comment speed_unit_update The km/h after the speed: state 0 sets it up (animation 10 at 127, CE), 1 draws it.
comment gear_indicator_update The gear at the bottom right, and the shifting: state 0 sets it up (rom_00D018 by the top gear: AUTO for the automatic). 1 each frame: the shift buttons (screen buttons_pressed bits 4 up, 5 down) change the gear within 0 and the top gear; shifting up at revs F000 or more, or down into revs past FFFF, spins the wheels 30 frames (car shift_spin, tyre noise 1); the gear's ratio to car gear_ratio, its frame drawn. Below the top gear with the revs at the limit (FF00) and the car moving, after 150 frames starts the shift_up_hint once. Once the race is over (car at_goal or unk_182), 2 only draws.
comment shift_up_hint_update A hint to shift up, blinking at the bottom centre (animation E): state 0 sets it up; 1 blinks it (4 frames of 8) while the car stays in the gear it started in with the revs at FC00 or more, then ends and lets the gear indicator start it again.
comment place_count_update The number of cars after the place at the bottom left (/2): ends at once with one player; state 0 sets it up (animation 5, frame the players less one) and starts the place_number; 1 draws it, and when the screen's place (screen place) changes passes the new one to the place_number (its place, state 2).
comment place_number_update The screen's place, 1st or 2nd, at the bottom left: state 0 sets it up (animation 4, the place's frame and colour); 1 draws it; 2 a new place: twice the size and blinking for 40 frames, 4 shrinking back into place in 8, then 5 draws it.
comment lap_timer_update The lap time after LAP at the top right: state 0 sets it up (the label, the text record and the checkpoints passed); 1 shows the race time since the last checkpoint (car race_time / 10 less car checkpoint_time, time_to_text); when the screen's car passes a checkpoint (screen 480 + player) the time it took (screen 3C0 records) starts the next lap (car checkpoint_time) and stays shown in another colour for 180 frames (split_frames; state 2), and C00074, which the board does not map, gets 8 + 2 * screen + (1 - the stage's parity); after the last (rom_00D0C0) 3 keeps the last lap's time.
comment start_message_update The start's messages in the middle of the screen: state 0 shows YOU'RE IN CAR NUMBER n! (animation 28F + the screen's car number, screen car_number, with DJ message 21 + it); when the start's countdown (the car object's unk_10) is at 30 frames, Ready (animation 31, DJ message 31); at 0, Go! (animation 32, DJ message 32), 30 frames, then it ends.
comment time_left_display_update The time left in seconds at the top centre, and the time up: state 0 waits for the race's time (unk_308's +6A), takes it into car time_left and sets the digits up (time_digits_start); 1 shows it during the start's countdown; 2 follows the race's time (until the screen's car has finished, race_copy finished) and starts EXTEND TIME at each checkpoint (time_left_display_check_extension); at 0 the time is up (car time_up) and 3 shows 0, until time comes back (the car drives on: its state 2). The digits flip as they change (time_digits_draw).
comment time_left_display_check_extension Starts the EXTEND TIME message once for each checkpoint passed (screen forks_decided, counted in car extensions_shown).
comment time_digits_start Sets up the three digits of the time at the top centre (animation 3 at A9, 11), each to open from nothing at its first value. Used by time_left_display, car_select_run and 001EC2.
comment time_digits_draw Draws the three digits of R0 (binary-coded decimal), the units at the right: a digit that changes squeezes shut and opens again as the new one; leading zeros are left out.
comment extend_time_message_start Sets up the EXTEND TIME message under the time (animation 33) for 90 frames, blinks one of the screen's cabinet lamps 16 times (bytes C1-C3 of its entry at checkpoint_lamp), then extend_time_message_blink.
comment extend_time_message_blink Blinks EXTEND TIME (4 frames on, 4 off) until its frames run out.
comment goal_update The goal line and what follows it: state 0 waits until the screen's car reaches its segment (position), then marks the car at the goal (car at_goal); 1 when the car's finish is recorded (race finished) sets up Congratulations! (animation 2F5) and the TOTAL TIME (the text at D08E and the screen's finish time, screen 410, through time_to_text), DJ message 34 when first, else 35, the screen's sound byte to 7F; 2 waits; 3 after 120 frames draws them for 60, then the title Dream Flying (start_song_title) and sound 10; 4 240 more frames of both, or with two players 5 only the TOTAL TIME beside the race_result; then it ends.
comment race_result_start With two players, the result at the end of the race: the screen's place in big letters (animation 2F8: 1st, 2nd) at the bottom for 240 frames (race_result_hold), and the verdict above it (race_result_verdict_start).
comment race_result_hold Draws the place or Loser until its frames run out.
comment race_result_verdict_start The verdict over the place: Winner (animation 2F6), blinking (race_result_verdict_blink), for the first, Loser (2F7) for the other, 240 frames.
comment race_result_verdict_blink Blinks Winner (4 frames on, 4 off) until its frames run out.
comment branch_arrow_update The arrow of the way taken at a fork, at the bottom centre, with CHECK POINT scrolling on the LCD between arrows of that way: state 0 sets it up (animation 34 or 35 by the branch, car branch; DJ message 33 when first, else 3A, unless one is playing) for 57 segments of the race (leave_segment); 1 cycles its colours (palette_list); 2 then grows and drops out of the screen. Ends when the race is over.
comment checkpoint_board_update The times at a checkpoint, in rows under the top line: state 0 takes each player's car image (rom_00CFC8 by the car chosen); 1 waits until the screen's car passes a checkpoint (screen 480 + player); 2 then shows a row for each car that has passed it, in the order they did (screen 390): the first with its time, the others with how far behind the first they were (BEHIND); each row slides in from the right, the screen's own blinks while it waits for the others, and all slide out once the race has gone C0 segments on (leave_segment) or is over; when the last has gone, back to 1.
comment checkpoint_board_draw_row Draws row unk_12 of the board for player R21, slid by its offset: the car number, the car's small image, the time text (draw_text_at; another colour after the first row) and, after the first, BEHIND.
comment checkpoint_board_leader_time Writes the first car's time R0 as M'SS"HH into player R1's row text.
comment checkpoint_board_gap_time Writes how far behind the first player R1 passed (R0, hundredths) into its row text, as time_to_text does but with spaces for zero minutes and zero tens of seconds.
comment time_to_text Writes the time R0 (hundredths of a second) as M'SS"HH, closed by 0, at R10. Used by lap_timer, goal, checkpoint_board, 001EC2 and 00270C.
comment race_cars_start Starts the race's cars: for each player of the race, a race_car with its race record and the tables of its model (body, driver_places, passenger_places, sprays, tag), the two race_car_occupant objects and two rider_gesture objects that follow it; then ends.
comment race_car_occupant_update One of the two people in a race car (rom_01440E, rom_01442E by occupant), seen only while the car shows an animation of its own (its special_body: a crash): then drawn at the car's place and size until its animation ends, when the second one lets the car go back to its body.
comment race_car_draw Draws a car of the race, the screen's own or a rival, from its race record (record), as the screen's view sees it: its world position less the view's through the view's matrix and scaled by its depth (off the screen, too near or too far: not drawn); its body (animation, frame, palette from the record, plus the screen's brake lights and wheel_turn), its driver and passenger (driver_places, passenger_places), the sprays at its wheels (race_car_draw_spray), five frames of animation 44 on its hard acceleration (record hard_acceleration, car hard_acceleration); over the screen's own car the CRASH (or Ouch!) text after a hit, over a rival its number tag, blinking. A rival behind the window of the road (within 256 segments) is shown small at the bottom of the screen, larger as it closes in and across as it is (with its tag). A body animation without wheels (rom_009E4C) sets special_body for the occupants. Once the race is over (screen flags bit 4) it becomes race_car_start_ending.
comment race_car_draw_spray Draws the spray at one side's wheels (dust, smoke, water...: the record's kind, from the race record's wheel effects), cycling its frames.
comment race_car_draw_part Draws a part of the car (a wheel, a spray) at its offset from the car's place, scaled with the car.
comment race_car_put_frame Puts the first piece of frame R14 of animation R12 with palette R13 into the sprite list, scaled by R8 at R17, R18 (race_car_put_sprite).
comment race_car_put_sprite Writes one sprite of a frame's piece into the screen's sprite list (and its copy at + 3000), its size and offsets scaled by R8, at R17, R18.
comment race_car_put_flat_sprite Writes the first piece of animation R12 into the sprite list at R17, R18 at its own size (the number tags).
comment race_car_start_ending The end of the race for the screen's car: starts the objects of its ending (object_lists_14A20 by its model and whether it won, or object_list_14A60 when it did not finish); when they are done (state 2) sound 4F and raises screen flags bit 1, which game step 7 waits for ( the music stopped without a finish).
comment rival_warning_update RIVAL SEKKIN !! (a rival approaching) at the centre of the screen: 12 frames on, 6 off, 12 on, with the rival_alarm, then it ends.
comment rival_alarm_update The sound of the rival warning: sound 2B twice, 15 frames apart.

# ======== the attract mode, the name entry, the ranking and the route map (naming batch E1) ========
001687  func  car_presentation_update
001980  func  car_name_start
0019C0  func  car_name_letter_update
001EC2  func  name_entry_update
002204  func  name_entry_letter_update
0022F7  func  name_entry_lcd_update
002379  func  name_entry_car_update
0023C6  func  name_entry_drivers_update
002416  func  name_entry_course_name_update
00247A  func  ranking_update
00254D  func  ranking_background_update
002612  func  ranking_title_left_update
00265F  func  ranking_title_right_update
0026AD  func  ranking_course_banner_update
00270C  func  ranking_record_start
00278E  func  ranking_record_character_update
002870  func  ranking_record_car_update
002933  func  passing_traffic_start
002952  func  passing_traffic_update
002998  func  passing_car_update
002AC8  func  ranking_background_load
003520  func  route_map_update
003E1B  func  route_map_car_update
003F27  func  route_map_sign_update
004056  func  route_map_landmark_update
004122  func  map_depth_from_y
004152  func  stage_result_line_draw
011011  func  queue_tile_pages
0119FA  func  draw_text_at
011E2C  func  start_object_list
0179AC  func  set_up_background
comment car_presentation_update The presentation of a car in the attract mode (game mode 3, object_handlers_1A78): the picture of the car screen attract_car names. By state: 0 starts car_name and staff_credits_code (with this object as its presentation, kept in staff_credits_code), the screen's background (set_up_background with 001AA0 by screen), the picture (animation rom_001A88 by the car, palette 275, draw mode 0) at x A0, y 60 on depth layer 0; 1 steps its animation (animation_advance) and draws it. Every frame, in all states, it queues the next of the four palette records of rom_001AC8 (palette_step, round and round) with queue_palette_list, so the picture's colours cycle. State 2 (the palette only, no picture) is not set by its own code.
comment car_name_start Starts the letters of the presented car's name (rom_001CEC by screen attract_car: MAD POWER, SPEED BUSTER, EASY HANDLING, SMOOTH OPERATOR, BAD BOY, ROAD MONSTER, QUICK REACTOR, WILD CHASER): a car_name_letter for each character, spaces too, 18 pixels apart from x 168, each 2 frames later than the one before (delay 15, 17...); then ends.
comment car_name_letter_update A letter of the presented car's name (font 346 at size A0, its colours cycling through 001D70 with step_palette, at y 14). By state: 0 sets itself up (its character from character, x + 8); 1 hidden until delay runs out; 2 slides in from the right, 24 pixels a frame for 14 frames (slide_frames); 3 stays until 120 frames after its start (frames_left); 4 slides out to the left, 24 pixels a frame, and ends once x is negative.
comment name_entry_update The name entry after a race won at the goal under the course's record time (game step 18, when course_record_beaten says so): the certificate (CONGRATULATIONS!, the course, BEST RUNNER OF THE RACE.) with the time and the name, a drum of letters to pick from. By state: 0 lights the screen's two lamps (unk_F20) and clears lcd_restart_frames, starts name_entry_lcd, the background (set_up_background with 002E4C by screen; start_palette_fades with 13F6C0), the countdown number (time_digits_start), object_handlers_2D58 (name_entry_car, name_entry_drivers, name_entry_course_name, passing_traffic) and the 32 name_entry_letter objects; open, timer 600 frames, wheel_angle A80, the name's text record at x F8, y 54 and the time's at x A8, y 54 (time_to_text of the player's time, screen 410 + 2 * player); 1 the drum spins in (wheel_angle + 20 a frame for 40 frames, spin_frames; a letter is 80 of the turn's 1000); 2 the wheel turns the drum a letter at a time, 4 frames a letter (turn_steps; a turn under way turns back if the wheel goes the other way), sooner the further it leans (turn_delay counts down by rom_002D6C by the wheel), and << and >> a letter each time; the letter in front is letter, and shows blinking at the name's next place; the gas pedal let up (under 40) then pressed (C0 or more), START or the music button enters it with a sound (rom_002D98 by place): the left arrow takes the last letter back, the right arrow enters a space, ED or the third letter ends the entry, as does the timer running out (the seconds left show as the countdown number, time_digits_draw); then open 0, an empty name or one of the names at 002D7C (SEX, XXX, XX and X's with spaces) becomes ---, 60 frames; 3 writes the record into the race record (unk_11 + 1, unk_16 the time, unk_12 the car's model, unk_13-15 the name, unk_18 the course, screen built_stage - 15), turns the lamps off and sets the game's next step (screen game_mode byte 2 = 0x15 (step 21)); 4 nothing. Every frame it draws the time and the name (draw_text).
comment name_entry_letter_update A letter on the name entry's drum (font 2DE; index 0-31: A-Z ! ? . and the right arrow, the left arrow and ED, rom_002D00); ends when the entry closes (the name entry's open is 0). By state: 0 its angle index * 80 round the drum, y_offset -5; 1 for 40 frames (spin_frames) y_offset + 1, the drum dropping into place. Every frame its place from its angle less the wheel_angle (the sine table at 100C00, the cosine at 101400): x_offset, depth, a width that narrows toward the drum's edges (width_factor), mirrored (flags bit 6) with palette 27 where it faces away (the cosine under 2000, more than 60 degrees from the front), 34 in front, 30 for the letter in front (shown 4 frames of 8: hidden, flags bit 5 cleared, when bit 2 of game_frame_count is clear); drawn in perspective (100000 / (depth + 100)) about x E0, y 40.
comment name_entry_lcd_update Shows the name entry on the cabinet LCD: 0 clears it (clear_lcd), shows the DJ's face (screen dj_face = FF) and writes the time (7 characters of the entry's time_text) at F13; then every frame the name's three characters at F1C, in the LCD's codes (7F -> 7E and | -> 7F, its arrows; \ -> 65); ends when the entry closes. It means to write spaces after the end of the name, but tests the address (tb R10) instead of the character, so it never does.
comment name_entry_car_update The player's car on the certificate screen (animation rom_002D9C by the car's model, car model; palette rom_002DAC) at x 40, y C0: state 0 sets it up and runs into name_entry_drivers_update, which with state 1 draws it; then it draws itself every frame.
comment name_entry_drivers_update The car's two drivers standing by it on the certificate screen (animation rom_002DBC by the car's model, car model; palette rom_002DCC) at x 60, y C8: state 0 sets them up and runs into name_entry_course_name_update, which with state 1 draws them; then drawn every frame.
comment name_entry_course_name_update The course's name on the certificate (Australia Course...: animation rom_002E24 by the course, screen built_stage - 15; palette rom_002E38) at x E0, y 40: state 0 sets it up; drawn every frame.
comment passing_traffic_start Counts the steps of the passing cars' path (0067B2: size, x, y; closed by 0) into path_steps, then runs passing_traffic_update.
comment passing_traffic_update Now and then a car passes on the road behind the certificate: by state, 0 one frame in 16 at random (random_number) starts a passing_car on the path's last step (path_steps), 1 then waits 10 frames (wait).
comment passing_car_update A car on the road behind the certificate (animation rom_002DDC by a random model, palette rom_002DAC; its sound, rom_0067A9 by the model, requested as it starts): state 0 picks frame C and state 1, or frame 1 and state 2 when the random number is negative; then a frame a step it goes from the path's last step (0067B2: size 370 near the bottom edge) to its first (size 5 on the horizon), at the path's x in state 1 or at 96 - x on the other side of the road in state 2; ends past the first step.
comment ranking_update The ranking in the attract mode (game mode 5, game_mode_ranking): OutRunner of OutRunners, the record of three courses in turn. By state: 0 starts object_handlers_2CB8 (01AA01, 01A6CF, which game_mode_ranking has started too), ranking_background and the two halves of the title (ranking_title_left, ranking_title_right); 1 clears course_over; once the screen's fade is over (screen flags bit 0): after three courses ends, else courses_shown + 1, 60 frames (frames_left), and starts ranking_course_banner and ranking_record for the course screen record_course; 2 when the 60 frames are over sets course_over for a frame (the background fades out) and moves screen record_course to the next of the ten courses, back to 1.
comment ranking_background_update The picture of the course behind the ranking, while the game mode is the ranking (screen game_mode 5). By state: 0 courses_shown from the ranking; for the first course I/O C0001C = 6 (C8001C = 86 for screen 2) and a fade in (screen fade_kind 1, unk_94 bit 0); ranking_background_load; 1 waits while the pages are unpacked (screen unpack_state), then for the later courses a fade in (unk_153 3); 2 when the ranking says course_over: after the third course a fade out (unk_153 0) and state 4, else a fade out (unk_153 2); 3 when the fade is over, back to 0 for the next course's picture; 4 nothing more. Every frame, in any game mode, the picture scrolls by 3 pixels (scroll_x - 3).
comment ranking_background_load Loads the picture of the course screen record_course (rom_002D20 picks it) into the screen's first tilemap: its register addresses for the screen (002C44, into 80-AC), the tile bank (rom_002C18), the palette (rom_0318CC, queue_palette_list), the four tilemap pages (rom_109694, with the screen's page numbers from rom_002CAC, in page_list; queue_tile_pages), start_palette_fades with rom_031B14, zoom 1:1, zoom centre and the second tilemap's scroll 0, the pages, the scroll's start (rom_002CB4: x 360 for screen 1, 4A0 for screen 2; y 60), its priority 1 and the second tilemap off in the mixer (rom_002CA4: screen 182 and 186; unk_94 bit 2); send_video_registers = FF to send the registers.
comment ranking_title_left_update The left half of the ranking's title, OutRunner of OutRunners (animation 2D2, palette 2B2) at x 0, y 30: state 0 sets it up; drawn every frame.
comment ranking_title_right_update The right half of the ranking's title, OutRunner of OutRunners (animation 2D3, palette 2B3) at x 13F, y 30: state 0 sets it up; drawn every frame.
comment ranking_course_banner_update The scroll with the course's name under the ranking's title (Australia Course...: animation rom_002DFC by course, palette rom_002E10) at x A0, y 50, for 90 frames (frames_left).
comment ranking_record_start Sets up the course's record line from the records at 20E300 (by course: the initials, '-' shown as '.', and a 0 into text, then the time as 5'00"00, time_to_text), starts a ranking_record_character for each of its 11 characters (index, course, itself) and ends at once.
comment ranking_record_character_update A character of a course's record line (font 2DE, palette 27): 0 its character from the record's text by index, at y 80 and x -A0 plus 16 pixels for each place of index; 1 slides in, 32 pixels a frame for 7 frames (slide_steps), then starts a ranking_record_car (each of the 11 characters starts one, all at the same place); 2 index + 2 frames later (colour_delay) takes the colours of the record's car (rom_002DEC by its model); 3 58 frames after its start (frames_left) slides out to the left, 16 pixels a frame, and ends at x -20.
comment ranking_record_car_update The record holder's car under the course's record line (animation rom_002DDC by the model of the course's record, palette rom_002DAC; size 50, y 88): drives in from the left 16 pixels a frame to x 108 or more, turns round (frame 9 down to 4), waits 15 frames (wait_frames), drives back left 16 pixels a frame and ends at x 0. State 5 (drawn only) is not set by its own code.
comment route_map_update The map of the route after the race (game step 12, object_handlers_34EC); keeps "Thank you for your playing." scrolling on the LCD (F40 = 005014). By state: 0 copies its screen's tilemap register addresses (0047E8) into 80-BC, first_branch from screen route_branches, the background (set_up_background with 004868 by screen and first_branch: the routes on tilemap 0 over the world on the second), the palette rom_031DDC[2C] (queue_palette); 1 zoom 1:1 about the middle of the screen, sea_colour black, 30 frames (timer); 2 the world's tilemap off, sea_colour 7EE0; 3 zooms in (zoom + 20 a frame for 9 frames, 200 being 1:1), then the close-up map (pages rom_0048B8) at its start (x scroll rom_0048D0 by first_branch, y 8C), starts route_map_car and the 32 route_map_landmark objects; 4 checkpoint_legs_left from the car's unk_D0, unk_12 from screen current_stage_level, stage 0; 5-14 the car drives the route leg by leg (leg_frames frames each): 22 frames up from the start to the first checkpoint (the car moves, the map stands), then 6 frames of 8 pixels sideways (first_branch picks the side), 11 of 5, then for each stage 10 frames of 4 up or down by the branch taken (screen route_branches + stage - 1) and 15 of 7 sideways; on these legs the map scrolls under the car (the object's x and y move the other way, for the objects on the map), screen route_map_signals telling the car its way (bits 0 down, 1 up, 2 right, 3 left, 5 the map at its edge, 7 standing); a stage_result_line starts where each stage begins (5, 7, 11), a route_map_sign (C.P) and stage + 1 where a checkpoint leg ends; when its count of legs (unk_12 for the first leg of a stage, checkpoint_legs_left for the others) is 0 at a leg's start, the race ended on it: last_leg_progress and leg_frames are worked out from the race_copy record's unk_42, the car's unk_4A and the stage lengths at 700 of the screen, and unk_5800 bit 6 ends the drive after it; at the end (stage 6 or a cut leg) the car stands (bit 7), FINISH (route_map_sign 1) if the goal was reached (race_copy finished), 60 frames; 15 the routes (pages rom_0048C0) at zoom 2x where the close-up map was, the steps of the zoom out into x and y, unk_5800 bit 4 (the car, the landmarks and the signs end); 16 zooms out over 9 frames to 1:1 and the whole route (x scroll rom_0048D4 by first_branch, y 70), then the world's tilemap again (pages rom_0048C8); 17 sea_colour black, starts 00463C and an 004240 for each leg (stage + 1, at most 6), DJ message 38, 90 frames; 18 DJ message 39 once the DJ is quiet (screen dj_mouth_shapes); 19 when the 90 frames are over sets the game's next step (screen game_mode byte 2 = 0x10 (step 16)) and starts 0046BB; 20 nothing.
comment route_map_car_update The player's car on the route map (animation rom_0048E0 by the car's model, palette rom_0048F0, size 50): 0 at x 8C, y C3 with frame C; 1 until the route map's stage is not 0, drives up 4 pixels and right 1 a frame unless the map says it stands (screen route_map_signals bit 7); 2 stays where it is while the map scrolls under it, its frame by the way the map says it goes (bit 2 frame 9, bit 3 frame 4, bit 0 frame 6, bit 1 frame C), moving itself 7 pixels a frame sideways when the map is at its edge (bit 5); ends when the map zooms out (bit 4). Its depth layer follows its y (map_depth_from_y).
comment route_map_sign_update A sign on the route map, by kind: 0 C.P where the car passed a checkpoint (animation 2F9), 1 FINISH at the goal (2FA); palette 2CF, depth layer 195. State 0: x_offset and y_offset are the middle of the screen (A0, 70 less 8) less the map's place, where the car is; kind 1 goes to state 2; 1 follows the map (its x and y plus the offsets) and turns (animation_advance); 2 FINISH at the goal's place on the map (the map's x plus rom_004042 by the goal, screen current_stage - 15; y 5C), drawn 4 frames of 8 (blink); both end when the map zooms out (screen route_map_signals bit 4).
comment route_map_landmark_update One of the 32 animated pictures on the close-up route map (people, animals and vehicles by the landmarks): 0 from its record (004C14 + 16 * the index in 9, the second set of 32 (+ 200) when screen route_branches is 1): animation, palette, size, x_offset and y_offset from the map's place, then field 9 from the record (set for one record: it then shows only every other frame); 1 follows the map (its x and y plus the offsets), animation_advance and draw_object, with field 9 set only when toggle comes round; ends when the map zooms out (screen route_map_signals bit 4). Its depth layer follows its y (map_depth_from_y).
comment stage_result_line_draw A line of the player's places by stage on the route map, with two players or more (ends at once with one): every frame, the stage's label (004BA4 by stage: PRESTG, STAGE1-4, RESULT) at x A8, y 40 + 16 * stage, and at x E8 the player's place at that stage (004BDA by screen 360 + player + 8 * stage: 1st-8th); the last line says Retire instead, unless it is RESULT (the goal reached), where the player's final place (place_address) is drawn big (animation 4, draw mode 8, at x F0, y B0, size 160; palette 41 for 1st, 42 for the others).
comment map_depth_from_y Sets the object's depth layer (3C) from its y: (y + D0) >> 3, at most 19F, so that what stands lower on the route map is drawn in front. Used by route_map_car and route_map_landmark.
comment draw_text_at Draws the string at R18 as draw_text does (a sprite per character into the screen's sprite list), at the place R3 holds (x in its low half, y in its high half, as a text record's first 32 bits) with the attribute R5. Used by stage_result_line, stage_indicator_draw and song_title_show (the song title).
comment queue_tile_pages Queues the tilemap pages of the list R10 to be unpacked into video RAM: each 32-bit entry a page number in its top byte (the page at 300000 + 400 * page) and the address of its packed data below, closed by a negative entry; the requests go into the screen's ring of 64 at C00 (unk_B03 the place) and unk_B00 says there is work. Used by set_up_background, ranking_background_load and race_background_update.
comment set_up_background Sets up the screen's two tilemaps (0 and 2 for screen 1, 1 and 3 for screen 2; their register copies listed at rom_017A78 by screen) from the 20-byte record R19: their pages (two words), their tile banks (two bytes) and their priorities in the mixer (two bytes, the low four bits of mixer registers 22 and 26, 24 and 28 for screen 2; 0 is off), zoom 1:1, zoom centre and scrolls 0, the second tilemap's row scroll and row select off (video_registers[2]), the registers and the mixer to be sent (send_video_registers, screen flags bit 2); then queues the pages of the list at the record's +C (queue_tile_pages) and the palette record at +10 (queue_palette_list). Used by car_presentation, name_entry, route_map, car_select_run, entry_countdown_start and title_screen_update.
comment start_object_list Starts an object for each handler of the list R10 (a 32-bit count, then the handlers: the object_handlers_* tables), each with its place in the list at 9, and returns the last one. Used by the game modes (game_mode_car_presentation, game_mode_ranking, game_mode_play, initialise_board...), name_entry and ranking.

# ======== the car select, the map after the race and the entry screens (naming batch E2) ========

comment turntable_draw Places the object at a point of the car select's turntable scene and draws it: R1 across, R2 down and R3 in depth (the turntable's centre at 140), seen from a view that screen turntable_depth_offset takes back (car_select starts it at -300 and raises it to 0, so the scene shrinks into its place): with d = R3 + 100 + turntable_depth_offset, x = R1 * (100000 / d) >> 12 + A0, y = R2 * (100000 / d) >> 12 + 60, the depth layer (3C) 100 - d >> 4, the width and height scale (100000 / d) * the object's size (1C) >> 12. Not drawn (flags bit 5 cleared) with d under 10 or x outside -28..168. Used by car_attendant, turntable_car and turntable_scenery.
comment queue_palette Queues a block of colours (record R19: its place in the palette, its length in bytes, the colours) for the screen's palette RAM, copied in the vertical blank (upload_queued_palettes).
comment queue_palette_list Queues a list of colour blocks (record R19: its length in bytes, then 8-byte entries: the colours' address, their place in the palette, how many blocks of 16) for the screen's palette RAM, copied in the vertical blank (upload_queued_palettes).
comment queue_palette_upload Adds R19 (bit 31 from R8: a list, not a block) to the screen's queue of palette uploads (the count at unk_900, the entries after it, up to 128).
comment upload_queued_palettes Copies the queued palette uploads (queue_palette, queue_palette_list) into the screen's palette RAM in the vertical blank and empties the queue.
comment upload_palette Copies a block of colours (place, length in bytes, colours) into the screen's palette RAM (600000 for screen 1, 680000 for screen 2).
comment upload_palette_list Copies a list of colour blocks (the list's length in bytes, then the address, place and number of 32-byte blocks of each) into the screen's palette RAM.
comment route_map_leg_show A leg of the route the player drove, drawn in red over the map shown after the race (003520), by state: 0 sets it up by its number (leg): 0 from the start to the first fork, 1 on from there by the direction taken (screen route_branches: 0 east, 1 west, the map's 09), 2-5 by the branch taken at each fork (screen route_branches + leg - 1) from the place of its stage (rom_004900, rom_00492A by screen route_stages + leg - 2), the map shifted by the direction (rom_004954) and 6B down; a leg driven already shows whole (frame 7, or B for 2-5), the leg the car is on (the map's unk_28) up to the frame before its progress, with the car on it (route_map_leg_car); legs 1-5 start the CP sign at their start (route_map_checkpoint), and leg 5, when the race reached the goal (race_copy finished), also the FINISH sign and the car at its end; 1 draws it.
comment route_map_leg_car_show The player's car on the map after the race, where the red line of its leg ends, by state: 0 sets it up: the sprite of its model (rom_0048E0, rom_0048F0 by car model) at a quarter size, its place and frame (which way it faces) from a record by the leg's progress (004964 for leg 0, 0049A4 for leg 1 by the direction, 004A24 for legs 2-5 by the direction and the branch, from the stage's place), shifted like the legs; 1 draws it.
comment route_map_checkpoint_show A sign on the map after the race, by state: 0 sets it up by its kind (rom_00403A, rom_00403E): 0 the CP sign at the start of its leg (the fork it leaves from), 1 the FINISH sign beside the goal end of the last leg (rom_00462C, rom_004634 by the direction and the branch); 1 the CP sign turns (animation_advance) and is drawn; 2 FINISH blinks, drawn 4 frames out of 8 (blink).
comment route_map_direction_sign_show The WEST BOUND / EAST BOUND sign at the foot of the map after the race: a globe between the two, placed by the direction (screen route_branches) so that only the half of the way taken shows, by state: 0 sets it up below the screen; 1 rises 2 a frame to DC; 1 and 2 cycle its colours through four palettes from palette_base, one every 4 frames (colour_timer), and draw it.
comment route_map_wait_banner_show JUST A MOMENT PLEASE over the map after the race, while the game waits to go on, by state: 0 waits 32 frames (timer), then sets it up at the centre of the screen, tiny; 1 grows to full size in 15 frames; 2 drawn 15 frames; 3 hidden 5 frames, back to 2.
comment game_over_banner_show GAME OVER at the centre of the screen when a one-player race ends short of the goal, by state: 0 sets it up, tiny; 0 and 1 grow it to full size in 15 frames (timer); 2 draws it.
comment car_attendant_run The man who stands by the car in front on the car select's turntable, by state (rom_005534): 0 sets him up half a car round from the front (angle, radius, size); 1 stands beside the car in front (car model) until it changes, then 2 walks round the turntable, 20 a frame, to the front, facing his way (flags bit 6), and stands again; once the car is chosen (car_select car_chosen), 3 walks to one side of the car or the other by the transmission the wheel picks (side_offset to car transmission * 80) and stands there; once it is chosen (transmission_chosen), 4 strikes the pose for it (animation 267 automatic, 268 manual). Each frame he is drawn at his angle (plus side_offset) less the turntable's (car_select angle) with turntable_draw, and animated.
comment turntable_car_show One of the eight cars on the car select's turntable (car: its model and its place, car << 9), by state: 0 its sprite set and palette (rom_0069B8 by car); 1 placed by its angle less the turntable's (car_select angle), with the frame that shows it from there (rom_0069D8), round the centre (radius screen turntable_radius_offset + 140) with turntable_draw; once the car is chosen (car_select car_chosen) the car in front takes the palette 4 on if it is the chosen one (car model) and goes to 2, where draw_object draws it where it was.
comment turntable_scenery_show A ring of scenery round the car select's turntable (one of twelve groups, from 006A18: the crowd, the palm trees), by state: 0 its animation, palette, size_step, size and list of items (item_list: angle, radius and height of each, closed by a negative angle); 1 each frame takes the turntable's angle (car_select angle) and draws the items on the far side (their angle less it 480..B7F) with turntable_draw, keeping for each its previous_place and flags bit 5 in item_places for draw mode 0A's halfway copy; then animation_advance.
comment select_car_heading_show "To Select A Car," at the top of the car select, by state: 0 starts the four pieces of the instructions under it (select_instruction, records at 006DF6) and sets it up; 1 drawn 20 frames (timer); 2 hidden 10 frames; it ends as soon as the car is chosen (car_select car_chosen).
comment select_instruction_show A piece of the car select's instructions ("Turn your", the steering wheel, "Then foot the", the accelerator; piece_animation, palette and place from select_car_heading), by state: 0 starts its animation; 1 drawn and animated until the car is chosen (car_select car_chosen); 2 rises 2 a frame for 16 frames (timer), over the transmission choice; 3 drawn and animated until the transmission is chosen (transmission_chosen), when it ends.
comment transmission_choice_run The transmission choice of the car select: the AUTOMATIC and MANUAL panels (on the second tile layer, with the sprites automatic_panel and manual_panel following x and y), by state: 0 starts the two panels; 1 slides them down into view, 10 a frame for 14 frames (y, car_select second_scroll_y_address); 2 when the wheel picks the other transmission (car transmission against shown_transmission) queues the panels' colours for it (queue_palette_list 13F998 automatic, 13F95C manual); when it is chosen (car_select transmission_chosen) starts the driver couple (driver_couple) and 3 slides the panels aside, 10 a frame for 10 frames (slide_step, slide_frames; x, second_scroll_x_address), the chosen one to the half the couple leaves free; 4 stays. From 2 on, every frame, the colours of the transmission picked or chosen cycle: the next palette list of rom_006E30 (automatic, automatic_cycle) or rom_006E44 (manual, manual_cycle) every two frames.
comment automatic_panel_show The AUTOMATIC TRANSMISSION title of the left panel of the transmission choice, by state (rom_005B50): 0 starts its label (transmission_label_start_automatic) and sets it up; 1 cycles its colours (step_palette from palette_list) while the wheel picks automatic (car transmission 0), else palette 2AE; drawn at x_offset, y_offset less the choice's x, y (transmission_choice), which slide it. Nothing sets state 2 (drawing only).
comment manual_panel_show The MANUAL TRANSMISSION title of the right panel of the transmission choice, by state (rom_005BE4): 0 starts its label (transmission_label_start_manual) and sets it up; 1 cycles its colours (step_palette from palette_list) while the wheel picks manual (car transmission 1), else palette 2AE; drawn at x_offset, y_offset less the choice's x, y (transmission_choice), which slide it. Nothing sets state 2 (drawing only).
comment driver_couple_start Sets up the couple who drive the chosen car (a man and a woman, by the car's model: 006E58) on the left (x 50) after automatic (left_side) or on the right (F0) after manual, the half the panels slid away from; draw_object then draws it as its handler.
comment car_caption_show The caption of the car in front on the car select (EASY HANDLING, SMOOTH OPERATOR...: animation 26E, frame car model, palette rom_006DDE), by state: 0 starts the gearboxes under it (car_gears_caption) and sets it up; 1 drawn 10 frames (timer); 2 hidden 10 frames; a new car in front (shown_car) shows it at once, for 10 frames more; it ends when the car is chosen (car_select car_chosen).
comment car_gears_caption_show The gearboxes of the car in front under its caption (Auto/5 Speed...: animation 2D1, frame rom_006E78 by car model, the manual box's), blinking like car_caption: 1 drawn 10 frames (timer), 2 hidden 10, at once for a new car (shown_car); it ends when the car is chosen (car_select car_chosen).
comment waiting_banner_show The car select's message once the choices are made, by state: 0 sets up WAITING FOR CHALLENGERS! JUST A MOMENT PLEASE (animation 28B); 1 drawn 20 frames (timer); 2 hidden 10; once the entry time is over (car_select entry_over) JUST A MOMENT PLEASE (animation 28D) instead: 3 drawn 20 frames, 4 hidden 10.
comment car_select_entries_run The other players on the car select, by state: 0 its list of the race's players (race_order: the count, then the players); 1 when the LCD is not scrolling a text (screen lcd_scroll) writes Entry: on it with _ at each player's car number (car_numbers); then every frame: this screen's number blinks with _ every 8 frames (own_number, digit_toggle, blink_timer); each other player not yet marked (a bit each from 40, the frame field) gets its number on the LCD and, once it has chosen a car (byte 48 of its race_copy record, which car_select writes), a flag over that car on the turntable (choice_flag, kept in car_flags by car; one more player on the same car gets a stacked_choice_flag on the last one) and its bit; flags_visible follows the blink pattern rom_006EA0 (frames >> 1).
comment choice_flag_start Sets up a flag over the car another player has chosen (animation 1B, a palette by the car, rom_006E80; angle: the car's place on the turntable), starts its number (choice_flag_number) and goes on to choice_flag_follow.
comment choice_flag_follow Keeps the flag over its car as the turntable turns (its angle less car_select angle, projected as turntable_draw does, above the cars) and draws it while car_select_entries' flags_visible says so (visible); not drawn behind the view.
comment stacked_choice_flag_start Sets up the flag of one more player on a car that has one (on the last flag there, below), starts its number (choice_flag_number) and goes on to stacked_choice_flag_follow.
comment stacked_choice_flag_follow Draws the flag 14 higher (and 3 right) than the one below it while that one is drawn (its flags bit 5 and visible); else it is not drawn.
comment choice_flag_number_start Sets up the car number of a flag's player (rom_006E90 by its car number, car_numbers), then choice_flag_number_follow.
comment choice_flag_number_follow Draws the number on its flag (13 right, 10 up from its place) while the flag is drawn (its flags bit 5 and visible); else it is not drawn.
comment entry_traffic_run Sends cars up the road behind the entry countdown (entry_traffic_car), by state: 0 one chance in 16 each frame to start one; 1 then waits 10 frames (delay).
comment entry_traffic_car_drive A car coming up the road toward the view behind the entry countdown (car: a random one of the eight, rom_0069B8), by state: 0 sets it up and picks its way: 1 along the path at 0067B2 (scale, x and y a frame, path_step) seen from the front and one side (frame 6), 2 the same path mirrored (x = 96 - x) from the other side (frame 7); one car in 128 goes to 3 or 4 instead, the same paths, but after 45 frames (spin_delay) its frames, its views all round, run five a frame (it spins) with set_tyre_noise(4) each frame; at the end of the path it requests its car's sound (rom_0067A9) and ends.
