comment title_screen_update The title of the attract mode. By state: 0 sets it up: clears screen 5800 and 5801 (title_logo_shine, the logo's shine signal), keeps the addresses of the tilemaps' register copies (01ACCC by screen: scroll_x_address, scroll_y_address for the first tilemap, second_scroll_x_address, second_scroll_y_address for the second), the background (set_up_background with 01AD0C by screen) and its scrolls, picks the car of the next car presentation (screen attract_car from unk_9E, which steps through the eight), starts the credits line (credit_display), the prompt (coin_prompt) and the eight cars driving past (title_car); 1 for 45 frames (frames): palm trees (title_screen_start_palms) and the two tilemaps scrolled by 8 and 20 a frame, then the logo (title_logo, title_logo_copy), three logo cars (title_logo_car) and the three lines across the logo (title_logo_stripe); 2 the same palms and scrolls, with three more logo cars at each count of wave_times (120, 240, 300, 390, 450).
comment title_screen_start_palms Starts the title's palm trees: a near one (title_palm_tree with near_or_far 0) every 8th frame (palm_frames), else a far one (1) every other frame.
comment title_car_drive One of the eight cars driving past at the bottom of the title (title_screen starts them, index 0-7). Sets itself up from its record (01ABB4 by index: the model, whose animation rom_01AC34 gives, palette, width_scale, height_scale, position, y, speed, and its depth_layer less 18F; draw mode A, frame 9) and starts its driver (title_car_driver) and passenger (title_car_passenger); each frame drives on by speed along a strip 4096 wide that wraps (clearing flags bit 5 as it wraps), is drawn at the position less the screen's offset (rom_01ABA4 by screen car_number) and bobs a line up on odd frames (frames; rest_y keeps its y meanwhile).
comment title_car_driver_update The driver of a title car (the blond man at the wheel): sets up its picture by the car's model (animation rom_01A012, palette rom_01A032, x_offset rom_01A062, y_offset rom_01A072, a layer in front of the car), then is drawn with the car each frame (title_rider_draw).
comment title_car_passenger_update The passenger of a title car (the red-haired woman beside the driver): sets up its picture by the car's model (animation rom_01A022, palette rom_01A042, x_offset rom_01A082, y_offset rom_01A092, a layer in front of the car), then is drawn with the car each frame (title_rider_draw).
comment title_palm_tree_update A palm tree passing the title from right to left (title_screen_start_palms starts them): near (near_or_far 0: full size, low on the screen, 48 a frame) or far (1: 3/8 of the size, along the beach, 18 a frame); ends once off the left edge.
comment title_logo_update The OutRunners logo of the title: slides in from the right (16 a frame for 10 frames); then starts the palm trees of its board (title_board_palms), the two seagulls (title_seagull) and the title's still pictures (title_picture: the board, SEGA, (C) SEGA 1992), flashes through the palettes at 01AC54 (palette_list) for 6 frames and settles in palette 269 at its next frame; then shines (its animation again from frame 1) each time the first logo car reaches the right edge (screen title_logo_shine).
comment title_logo_copy_update A copy of the logo in other colours (cyan) that slides in from the left as the logo (title_logo) slides in from the right, meets it, flashes through the palettes at 01AC62 (palette_list) and ends.
comment title_logo_car_update A small car racing across the logo along one of its lines (title_screen starts three at a time, index 0-2): after 1, 5 or 9 frames by index, one of the eight cars at random (rom_01AC34, rom_01AC44) at a quarter of its size at the left edge; 16 a frame to the right edge; the first of the three then makes the logo, its board's palm trees and the lines shine (screen title_logo_shine for a frame), and it ends.
comment title_logo_stripe_update One of the three thin coloured lines across the logo that the logo cars race along (index 0-2): after 1, 5 or 9 frames by index it slides in from the left (16 a frame for 19 frames), then shines with the logo (its animation from frame 0 when screen title_logo_shine is set).
comment title_board_palms_update The palm trees at the right end of the logo's board: grow in from 3/4 of their size (8 frames), then sway each time the logo shines (screen title_logo_shine).
comment title_seagull_fly One of the two seagulls over the title (index 0, 1): flies up out of the logo for 8 frames growing to full size, then circles about that place in a figure of eight (the sine of x_angle, a turn in 128 frames, and of y_angle, twice as fast), flapping (its animation).
comment title_picture_draw One of the title's still pictures (title_logo starts them): by index, from its record at 01AC70 (animation, palette, width_scale, height_scale, x, y, depth_layer): 0 (C) SEGA 1992, 1 the oval board behind the logo, 2 the SEGA logo.
comment coin_prompt_update The prompt at the bottom of the title, the car presentations and the demo races, and its text on the cabinet's LCD; ends at once in game mode 6 (screen game_mode). Every 30 frames (round_frames): a little car crosses the message (coin_prompt_car with this round's car; next_car then steps through the eight, from screen car_number) and the message by the seat (seat1_credits and seat1_coin_units for screen 1, seat2_credits and seat2_coin_units for screen 2): with free play (free_play) or a credit message 2, TURN THE IGNITION KEY (coin_prompt_start_key_prompt); else message 1 with coins in toward a credit or 0, a coin_prompt_message in the car's colour (INSERT COIN(S)). Each frame the LCD's text to scroll (screen lcd_scroll_request) is the message's (rom_01A9BC: Insert Coin(s), Insert More Coin(s), Turn the Ignition Key), except in game modes 6 and 7 or while a text scrolls (unk_F44).
comment coin_prompt_start_key_prompt Starts R1 pieces of the ignition key prompt (ignition_key_prompt), pieces R2 on, and returns the last one.
comment coin_prompt_car_drive The little car that drives across the prompt from right to left each round (coin_prompt starts it): one of the eight cars (car) at a quarter of its size, 14 a frame, ending at the left edge.
comment coin_prompt_message_update The prompt's message for a round (coin_prompt starts one every 30 frames): after 6 frames, INSERT COIN(S) (message 0; 1 when coins are in, animation rom_01A9A6) in the colour of the round's car (rom_01A9AC by car_colour), animating in for 22 frames over the one before, then a layer back for 30 frames, and ends; ends at once when the prompt's message changes.
comment ignition_key_prompt_update A piece of the prompt when a game can start (coin_prompt starts both every round): by piece, from its record at 01A95B (animation, palette, width_scale, height_scale, x, y, depth_layer, frames_left): 0 the words TURN THE IGNITION KEY, 1 the START / IGNITION key with its arrow; animates until frames_left runs out (24 or 30 frames) and ends.
comment credit_display_update The credits line under the prompt (title, car presentations, demo races); ends in game mode 6. With free play (free_play) FREE PLAY; otherwise, for the screen's seat (seat1_credits and seat1_coin_units for screen 1, seat2_credits and seat2_coin_units for screen 2): Credit (0 or 1) or Credits and the number of credits in the font's digits (tens at x AC when there are, units at B4), and while coins are in toward the next credit the coins and the coins a credit costs (coins_per_credit) as a fraction (C0 and C8), each drawn with credit_display_digit.
comment credit_display_digit Draws one digit of the credits line at x and y: digit_kind picks the animation and palette (rom_01AB98, rom_01AB9E: 0 the font, whose frames are its characters, 1 the coins, 2 the coins a credit costs) and digit the frame.
comment demo_captions_run The captions over the demo race of the attract mode: counts its frames and runs the routines of timeline_1AFC0 as it reaches their frames (30 demo_captions_head_to_head, 225 demo_captions_checkpoint, 420 demo_captions_music_select); ends once the count passes a record's frame (the closing FFFF).
comment demo_captions_head_to_head Frame 30 of the demo captions: HEAD TO HEAD! 2 PLAYER RACING (demo_caption records 0, 1 and 3 + machine_count, or 4 when stand_alone is set; 4 in attract.txt).
comment demo_captions_checkpoint Frame 225 of the demo captions: AT THE CHECKPOINT ONLY THE RACE LEADER CAN SELECT THE NEXT STAGE ! (demo_caption records 2 and 3).
comment demo_captions_music_select Frame 420 of the demo captions: the radio's panel, the hand pressing its button and MUSIC SELECT (demo_caption records 8-11).
comment demo_captions_start Starts R1 demo_caption objects with the records from R0 on.
comment demo_caption_update One picture of the demo captions (demo_captions_start starts them): from its record at 01AF00 (animation, palette, width_scale, height_scale, x, y, depth_layer, frames_left: 180 frames for records 0-7, 240 for 8-11), animated and drawn until frames_left runs out.
comment traffic_follower_update A vehicle in a line of road traffic behind a 0138EB leader (traffic_car_start_convoy starts the line from the leader's list: each one the list's spacing behind the one before it, with its own record): moves and draws as the leader's family does (traffic_car_update), but lives exactly as long as the one before it (leader): when that one has ended it ends too (screen traffic_count, the count of the traffic, less 1). The only line in the road's lists is in list 25 of object_lists_2E1F0 (a leader of animation 35C and eight of 35D); the scenarios start it only for a frame or two.
comment pulled_vehicle_update The vehicle a road animal pulls (traffic_figure_start_team starts it behind a 013BA3 leader from the leader's list): the covered wagons behind the horses (list 1 of object_lists_2E1F0), the sleighs behind the reindeer (lists 25 and 26). Moves and draws as the 013AF9 traffic does (traffic_stopping_car_update), and lives exactly as long as the one before it (leader): when that one has ended it ends too (screen traffic_count less 1).
comment traffic_hit_sound_wait Plays a road object's own sound 15 frames after the car has hit it (traffic_start_hit_sound starts it with the sound of the object's record): counts delay down, then requests sound (request_sound) and ends.
comment rider_gesture_update A rider's gesture on another racer's car (00974C; race_cars_start starts one for its driver, rider = model, and one for its passenger, model + 8; only some have a gesture, rom_01444E, the others end at once): when the car moves up a place in the race (rider_gesture_check_place) it plays its gesture once (the driver raises an arm, a finger up) in place of the rider, and while the car can be seen from the angle and the screen's car is racing (rider_gesture_check_view) it is drawn on the car (rider_gesture_place) at the car's size and a layer in front; then waits for the next time.
comment rider_gesture_check_view Whether the gesture shows (zero clear): only while the screen's car object is in state 2 or 4 (rom_0143A0) and the car is seen from one of the angles that show it (rom_0143A6 by the car's view); otherwise clears the car's gesture_shown; then rider_gesture_check_place.
comment rider_gesture_check_place Notes the racer's place in the race (screen + 320 + player) in its car's last_place and sets its gesture_shown when it is better than the last one; returns gesture_shown masked with R9 (zero set when there is no gesture to show).
comment rider_gesture_place Places the gesture on its car: the car's x and y (plus the screen's scroll, screen sprite_origin_x and sprite_origin_y) and the rider's offset, rom_014360 and rom_014380 by rider plus the seat's offset for the car's view (rom_18D25C by model, by the car's view, the driver's or the passenger's), scaled by R0 (the car's width_scale).
comment race_opening_update The opening of a race (not in the demo): the view comes down from high above the start line to behind the car, taking the car's unk_10C up by 18 and unk_10E down by 3 a step until it is 0 (80 steps from FA60 and F0; camera_step), while the opening's sounds play (race_opening_sounds_play; sound_step); ends when both are done.
comment race_opening_sounds_start Starts the opening's sounds: the first at count 1, the list at 014661.
comment race_opening_sounds_play Counts the opening's steps (count) and requests each sound of the list at its count (sound_list: sounds 2D, 24, 0A, 21, 2E, 2D, 24 at the counts 1, 20, 65, 120, 150, 160, 190); after the last goes on to race_opening_sounds_done.
comment race_opening_sounds_done Does nothing: the opening's sounds are over.
comment road_surface_update The road of the race, drawn on its tilemap with line scroll and line select. By state: 0 road_surface_start; then each frame this frame's lines (road_surface_lines) and, from state 2, those of the frame in between (road_surface_halfway_lines); where the first segment's page set (segment_page_set) is 0 it starts the stage's palette fades (start_palette_fades with screen road_palette_fades; state 3), and at the next one that is not takes it as page_set and fades back (road_surface_fade_back; state 2). Each frame the tilemap's page word (at page_register) is the page table's word for page_set and near_scroll (five a set). At each new segment (segment past last_segment) acts on the segment's event when it changes (after the first 30 segments, event_delay): 4 and 5 change the background within the stage (screen stage_background_change = FF, unk_204 = 0 or 1, for race_background), 1 and 2 turn the two road_tiles_change flags (0A, 0B) on and off, 3 both; a flag turned on starts a road_tiles_change (the Golden Gate Bridge in race_to_goal.txt, frames 7659-8325).
comment road_surface_start Sets up the road's tilemap from the screen's list at 01710C: its bits in video_registers[2], its scroll (160, 0), tile bank, mixer bits, page_register, the line tables in the video RAM (line_scroll_table, line_select_table) with the line select's start from 100800, the page set of the stage (rom_03CC80 by screen first_stage), the page table (from 01714C) and the stage's palettes (rom_031C38 by stage: the fades' list into screen road_palette_fades, the rest queued with queue_palette_list).
comment road_surface_lines Works out this frame's road: from the segments in view (screen + 3000, 16 bytes each, the first at the view's position race_copy window_segment) the first one's page set and event (+C, +D) and number (segment), each segment's point (+2 depth, +4 x, +6 y) into the points buffer (screen + 3800 by points_buffer), and between points the lines of the line scroll (the road's x) and line select (its depth, every other pair of segments in the picture's second half: the stripes) tables, from the bottom line up (road_surface_fill_lines); a stretch it passes over is filled after (road_surface_fill_skipped_lines); the farthest point's line into screen road_top_line and the bottom line's scroll into near_scroll.
comment road_surface_halfway_lines Works out the road of the frame in between: the same lines from the average of this frame's points and the last frame's (the two halves of the points buffer, matched by segment), into the next tables; flips points_buffer.
comment road_surface_fill_lines Fills the road's lines between two of its points, from the lower one up: the line scroll steps evenly from one point's x to the other's and the line select from one's depth to the other's (at most R18), plus R14 (100 on alternate pairs of segments); returns the two steps.
comment road_surface_fill_skipped_lines Fills the stretch of lines road_surface_lines passed over (from line skipped_line, at the table places it kept) by going on from the kept point with the kept steps down to line DF; nothing when there is none.
comment road_surface_start_tiles_change Starts the road_tiles_change of event 1 (its data at 017444, the even bytes of the page table) with the road's first flag (0A).
comment road_surface_fade_back Starts the palette fades of list R19 backwards (start_palette_fade from each record's second palette to its first): the road's colours back after a change of page set.
comment road_tiles_change_update Changes the road's tiles for a stretch of road (road_surface starts it when an event turns one of its flags on; the Golden Gate Bridge in race_to_goal.txt): copies the four tile pages the road shows (source_pages to target_pages, a page a frame), turns its half of the road's page table (table_half: the even or odd bytes) to the copies, copies the new look into the pages by the road's page set (16 halfwords of each a frame for 16 frames) and turns the table back; when the road turns its flag off (flag) it changes them back the same way and ends; its data list (data: 017444 for the first flag, 0174B4 for the second) holds the pages and the bytes for the table.
comment race_background_update The scenery behind the road (the race's first tilemap). By state: 0 sets it up: its register copies (017A50 by screen: scroll_x_address, scroll_y_address, page_word_address, tile_bank_address), the stage's background (background: rom_017B60 by screen first_stage, none for stage 0), its tile pages (rom_109694, loaded at once with unpack_tile_pages), tile bank and palettes (race_background_fade_palettes, queue_palette_list), its objects (race_background_start_objects) and, for stages 6, 8 and 1C, its animation (race_background_start_lower_animation, race_background_start_upper_animation); 1 waits for a change: a fork's new stage (the car's unk_CA; background 2 * screen built_stage + the branch, car branch) or the road's events 4 and 5 (screen stage_background_change; background 2A + unk_A3): screen background_changing set, the new background's pages queued into the other half of the pages (page_half; queue_tile_pages); 2 changes over as the view turns away and back (the car's unk_2E; change_step): when it starts its palettes, tile bank and the first page word (next_page_words), at 2A0 (off the screen) the fades of rom_0319F0 and of the road (rom_031CB8, into screen road_palette_fades) and the next, the new stage's objects (screen route_stage), at 160 its palette fades (race_background_fade_palettes) and the next, at 60 the last, at 0 back to state 1 with the animation of backgrounds 5, 7, 8, 25 and 26. Each frame scrolls it: x by the car's unk_2E (a quarter of it, all of it during a change) less A0, y by the slope (the car's unk_30) and screen horizon_line, kept between 0 and 180 less the road's top line (screen road_top_line; also into screen background_scroll_y).
comment race_background_start_lower_animation Loads the tiles of the background's lower animation (queue_tile_pages with rom_017904 by screen) and starts it (race_background_lower_animation).
comment race_background_start_upper_animation Loads the tiles of upper animation R0 (queue_tile_pages with rom_017988 by R0 and screen) and starts it (race_background_upper_animation).
comment race_background_start_objects Starts the background objects of stage R0 (object_lists_181B8; R0 + 1F during a change within the stage, screen stage_background_change), each with its data (field 80) and this object (14).
comment race_background_fade_palettes Starts the palette fades of list R19 (start_palette_fade for each record) and a race_background_fade_watch that keeps them; with an empty list clears screen background_fading (the background's palettes are changing) at once.
comment race_background_lower_animation_wait Waits until the animation's tiles are loaded (screen unpack_state), then goes on to race_background_lower_animation_cycle.
comment race_background_lower_animation_cycle Every 2 frames (delay) puts the next of four page pairs (rom_01790C; step goes two on, keeping to the screen's own) into the second half of the background's page word (the lower pages); ends when a change of background starts (screen background_changing).
comment race_background_upper_animation_wait Waits until the animation's tiles are loaded (screen unpack_state), then goes on to race_background_upper_animation_cycle.
comment race_background_upper_animation_cycle Every 3 frames (delay) puts the next of three page pairs (rom_0179A0) into the first half of the background's page word (the upper pages: the schools of fish over the underwater tunnel swim); ends when a change of background starts (screen background_changing).
comment race_background_fade_watch_update Waits for the background's palette fades (fades, count of them) to end, forgetting each as it ends (its flags bit 0 clear); then clears screen background_fading (the background's palettes are changing) and ends.
comment palette_fade_prepare Prepares a palette fade, one colour a frame for its 16 (colours_left): the colour of the first palette (from) into its colours (34 on) and its red, green and blue (five bits each) less those of the second (to) into its differences (54 on); then palette_fade_step.
comment palette_fade_step Every period frames (delay; start_palette_fade staggers the first) moves each of the 16 colours one step of each of red, green and blue toward the second palette (by its differences, counted down to 0) and queues them (queue_palette_list with its record at 28: 8 bytes, the colours, palette_offset, blocks); ends when no colour moved, or when the screen fades out (screen flags bit 0, with unk_153 at most 5 and even).
comment start_palette_fade Starts a palette_fade of 16 colours from the palette at R10 to the one at R11, for palette offset R12 (R0 blocks of 16 in its upload record), a step every R1 frames, the first after (screen palette_fades_started mod R1) + 1 (unk_F0E counts the fades started); returns the object.
comment start_palette_fades Starts a palette_fade for each 12-byte record of list R19 (a 32-bit length in bytes, then records: from, to, palette offset, blocks, period).
comment song_title_show A song title on the screen (start_song_title starts it, for car_radio, the car select and 00C8B6): its x and text from the record (x, and the text, which also scrolls on the LCD through screen lcd_scroll_request), then slides in from the right (slide from D2, slowing down to a stop by speed), stays for time_left (60 frames) and slides out to the left speeding up; sent away early (time_left 0, car_radio_show_title) it leaves at 40 a frame. Drawn each frame with draw_text_at at y C8 in colours that cycle every 2 frames (rom_019561 by colour_step).

# ======== the frame loops, the inputs, the game modes and the EEPROM (naming batch G) ========
0000C4  func  crc16
0000FC  func  signature_checksum

comment boot The reset's code (000006): SBR to the vector table, the stack 21FC00, 16 bytes from 0000A4 to E00000 and 4 from 0000B4 to E80000 (which the board does not map), the interrupt controller's registers from 0000B8, privilege level 0 with the stack 21F400 and a delay; then clears 21E000-21E3FF, turns the link board off (801000), clears 21F000-21FFFF, sets the I/O chips up (init_io_chips), clears the game's variables (20E000-20FFFF), writes the second task's first interrupt frame (000C98 to 21FF84: its PC 0006B0) and its stack pointers (waiting_task_frame, waiting_task_stack), initialise_game, lcd_start (the LCD), the interrupts on, the link's check (network_check), and goes on as the boot task's frame loop (second_task(1), screen 2's).
comment second_task The two tasks' frame loops, one function in the C: second_task(0) is the second task (from 0006B0), which runs screen 1; second_task(1) the boot task after boot (from 000637), which first sets up the link's race (link_take_master_settings), the interrupt controller's timer (D00008, D0000A), task_swapping, each screen's first attract car (screen next_attract_car from rom_000D14 by machine_count and the screen's car number) and the screens on (C0001C, C8001C), then runs screen 2. A frame of either: wait_for_frame (the VBLANK swaps the tasks meanwhile; the second task calls link_send_records before it with second_task_waiting set, and the boot task calls link_send_records and link_receive_records only while that is clear), the test mode when test_mode_on is set, link_receive_records, race_entries_update, race_progress_update and course_records_update (the link and the race records), game_frame_count + 1 (screen 1's loop), take_screen_inputs, check_game_start, the game mode's code (the table at 0008CC by game_mode: game_mode_power_on, game_mode_restart, game_mode_title, game_mode_car_presentation, game_mode_demo_race, game_mode_ranking, game_mode_play, entry_invitation_step (the entry countdown on a seat without a player), game_mode_isdn_connection), run_objects and lcd_update; with single_task set it goes on with the other screen's frame instead of waiting. The test mode: task_swapping off, test_mode_start, then test_mode_frame each frame while test_mode_on lasts; then the screens off, the variables cleared, the second task's frame written again, initialise_game, lcd_reset, both screens in game mode 1 with a fade in (fade 1), network_check_again, and the boot task's part again.
comment wait_for_frame Waits for the next frame: polls frame_ready (set by the VBLANK, which also swaps the tasks) and clears it; while the screen has tile pages to unpack (screen unpack_state) it unpacks them a piece at a time (unpack_queued_step) between the polls.
comment initialise_game Sets the game up, at start-up and after the test mode: 13, 0, 0, 1F to C00074 (which the board does not map); for screen 2, then 1, its objects' page cleared (clear_screen_ram) and its constants (init_screen: index, its video registers, the view's, the courses' stage lengths at 700); initialise_board; then initialise_screen for screens 1 and 2.
comment initialise_screen Sets a screen up: its sound off (reset_screen_sound), no objects (clear_objects), its tilemaps' registers and their copies (init_mixer), three palettes uploaded (upload_boot_palettes) and the courses' records (load_course_records).
comment initialise_board Sets the board up: the I/O chips (init_io_chips), the settings (load_settings), the sprite chip and its RAM (init_sprite_chip, load_sprite_ram_tables), the video RAM and its control (init_video), the backup RAM checked (bookkeeping_check), the credits (init_coins), the sound requests (start_sound_program), three lists of tile pages unpacked at once (unpack_tile_pages: 109420, 109428, 109578), then the inputs read and taken twice (read_switches_and_analog, take_screen_inputs) so that their previous values hold.
comment read_switches_and_analog Reads the inputs in the VBLANK (int_vector_40; twice at start-up): the switches of both I/O chips (ports E and F: bits 0-1 the seats' service switches, 2-5 the coin switches, 7 a test switch) into switches, with previous_switches, switches_pressed and switches_released; then, on the turn of the screen whose task runs, half the analog inputs through the A/D converter (C00050 + 4 * index): screen 1's turn seat 1's wheel, gas and brake and seat 2's wheel, screen 2's seat 2's gas and brake (the converter's second bank), each raw into analog_raw on and scaled to 0-FF between its calibration (a low and a high byte from 20E638, in the settings) into analog_inputs on; then the converter's bank for the other turn (C00060).
comment pack_seat_inputs Packs both seats' inputs for the coming frame (the VBLANK, on screen 1's turn) into their records: the screen's player + 1, the buttons (port A's high bits into 0-3, its low two into 4-5, the shift; port E bit 4 into bit 6, START), the wheel (analog_inputs inverted), the gas and the brake: seat_1_player to seat_1_brake and seat_2_player to seat_2_brake. On ISDN (isdn_mode) they go to the link board instead (link_send_seat_inputs).
comment link_send_seat_inputs Never runs in the scenarios (ISDN only): writes a seat's five bytes as halfwords on the link board at R11 (800020 on): 0, the buttons (R0), the wheel (inverted), gas and brake from analog_inputs at R10; or, when its screen has a link_event (R2: screen + 40), the event and the four bytes after it, which it then clears (without moving R10 on, so the next seat would read this one's analog inputs).
comment queue_seat_inputs queue_screen_inputs for screen 1 (seat_1_buttons, the first I/O chip) and screen 2 (seat_2_buttons, the second): the VBLANK on screen 2's turn.
comment queue_screen_inputs Puts a screen's inputs into its ring of eight (screen + 130, at input_write_place): the seat's buttons (R11) with ~port B of its I/O chip (R12 + 2) in bits 8-15; and the record's wheel, gas and brake into the screen's wheel, gas and brake. On ISDN the record is the screen's race_copy's +2C (the inputs the link brought).
comment take_screen_inputs Takes the screen's inputs for the frame from its ring (input_read_place; both frame loops, before check_game_start): buttons, with previous_buttons, buttons_pressed and buttons_released (bit 6 START, 4-5 the shift up and down, 0-3 the others, 8-15 port B). On ISDN also the link's status (race_copy link_status) into link_status, with link_status_set, link_status_cleared and link_status_changed, and, unless in it already, game mode 8 (game_mode_isdn_connection) while it says the line connects (bits 2-3).
comment check_game_start Each frame before the game mode's code: in the attract modes (not 0, not 6), with a credit on the seat (seat1_credits, seat2_credits for screen 2) or free play (free_play), blinks the START lamp (bit 2 of the seat's port_d_outputs, 8 frames on and 8 off by start_lamp_blink); START pressed (buttons_pressed bit 6) takes the credit (not with free play; and clears the bytes at 20E0A2 and 20E0AA, after each seat's coin units), turns the lamp off and starts a game: game mode 6 at step 0, link_event 1, the race record's car part cleared (race_record_clear) and race entry_request = FF. Returns the game mode.
comment game_mode_power_on Game mode 0 (the first frame after start-up): game mode 2 and its first step (game_mode_title).
comment game_mode_restart Game mode 1 (after the ranking, game_mode_ranking, and after the test mode): game mode 2 and its first step (game_mode_title).
comment game_mode_title Game mode 2, the title (title_screen_update), by mode_step: 0 the screen cleared (as clear_screen), the title's palette (031E88), title_screen_update started, a fade in (fade 1), the input ring's places to 0 (input_write_place, input_read_place), the screen on (C0001C = 6, C8001C = 86 for screen 2); 1 when the fade is over, mode_timer 450 frames (15 seconds); 2 counts them down, then a fade out (fade 0); 3 when it is over, game mode 3 (the car presentation). attract_lamps_update every frame first.
comment game_mode_car_presentation Game mode 3, the presentation of a car (car_presentation_update), by mode_step: 0 the screen cleared (as clear_screen), the palettes of the car's scene (rom_031DDC by rom_001A98 by attract_car) and 031740, object_handlers_1A78 (credit_display, coin_prompt, car_presentation) started, a fade in; 1 when it is over, mode_timer 180 frames (6 seconds; the staff credits code adds 1200); 2 counts them down, then a fade out; 3 when it is over, link_event 3 and game mode 4 (the demo race). attract_lamps_update every frame first.
comment game_mode_demo_race Game mode 4, the demo race of the attract mode (the car just presented, attract_car, on its stage): attract_lamps_update, then by mode_step demo_race_set_up, demo_race_fade_in, demo_race_start_timer, demo_race_count_down, demo_race_end, and but on the set-up's and the end's frames the race's own steps (game_mode_play by game_step, from step 10).
comment demo_race_set_up Step 0 of the demo race: demo_flags bit 8 (the race's code tells the demo by it: the car drives itself), the screen cleared (as clear_screen), step 1 with mode_timer 0, and game_step 10 (the demo race's set-up in game_mode_play, which does not run this frame).
comment demo_race_fade_in Step 1 of the demo race: after two frames (mode_timer), a fade in and step 2.
comment demo_race_start_timer Step 2 of the demo race: when the fade is over, mode_timer 600 frames (20 seconds) and step 3.
comment demo_race_count_down Step 3 of the demo race: counts mode_timer down, then a fade out and step 4.
comment demo_race_end Step 4 of the demo race: when the fade is over, game mode 5 (the ranking) and the race record's car part cleared (race_record_clear), without the race's step that frame.
comment attract_lamps_update The lamp show of the attract modes (game_mode_title, game_mode_car_presentation, game_mode_demo_race, game_mode_ranking, game_mode_isdn_connection): every 8 video frames (vblank_counter >> 3) the next of 32 steps of the pattern at 001607, two bytes a step and screen, into the screen's two lamp entries (byte 0 of checkpoint_lamp and leader_lamp by 4 * index: bits 1 and 3 of the seat's output port D, which the VBLANK writes).
comment game_mode_ranking Game mode 5, the ranking (ranking_update), after the demo race and after a game, by mode_step: 0 demo_flags bit 8 clear, the wheel motor off (wheel_motor by index), the sound off (reset_screen_sound), the screen cleared (as clear_screen), clear_published_car, the ranking's palette (031E80), object_handlers_2CB8 (credit_display, coin_prompt) and ranking_update started, mode_timer 270 frames (9 seconds), race game_over cleared; 1 waits until no fade runs (ranking_background fades the screen in); 2 counts the 270 frames down; 3 when no fade runs, game mode 1 (back to the title). attract_lamps_update every frame first.
comment game_mode_isdn_connection Game mode 8, on ISDN only (never in the scenarios; take_screen_inputs goes to it while link_status says the line connects, bits 2-3), by mode_step: 0 a fade out (attract_lamps_update in 0 and 1); 1 when it is over, no objects, the sound off, the screen's tilemaps, sprites and text off, palette 031740, the lamps off (checkpoint_lamp, leader_lamp by index), clear_lcd, isdn_status_update started (the list at 000F70); 2 a fade in; 3 when it is over, 4; 4 when link_status says connected (bit 0: isdn_count_players) or no longer connecting (bits 2-3 clear), mode_timer 150 frames; 5 counts them down, then a fade out; 6 when it is over, game mode 2 (the title).
comment isdn_status_update Never runs in the scenarios: the ISDN connection screen's texts (game_mode_isdn_connection starts it from the list at 000F70), on the text layer (print_text_layer), by state: 0 by link_status: bit 0 ISDN SUCCESS, THIS SIDE, OTHER SIDE and TOTAL with the players (race_copy players_this_side, unk_2A and their sum, print_hex_byte), then 1; bits 2-3 clear ISDN FAILED, then 1; else ISDN CONECTING (sic) blinking every 8 frames (its unk_10 counting); 1 nothing more.
comment isdn_count_players Never runs in the scenarios (ISDN only): the players on this side and on the other (race_copy players_this_side, unk_2A) into isdn_players_this_side and isdn_players_other_side, their sum into player_count (the race's players) and half of it into machine_count (its cabinets).
comment game_mode_play Game mode 6, a game (check_game_start starts it at step 0) and the demo's race (game_mode_demo_race, from step 10), by game_step: 0 sound 21 (queue_sound), the screen's two lamps on (unk_F20), lcd_restart_frames cleared (the LCD's timer), a fade out, race game_over cleared; 1 when the fade is over, waits until the screen's player is in the list of the race's players (from byte 1F of the race_copy record of host_player), then the host itself (not on ISDN) writes 1 to the link board's 80000A; 2 the car select: demo_flags bit 8 clear, the wheel motor off (wheel_motor), game_start_time from the backup RAM's seconds (701F10), a game counted on the seat (701F14 + 2 * index, bookkeeping_increment16), C00074 = rom_003463 by index, the sound off (reset_screen_sound) unless the entry countdown's music plays (entry_countdown_shown), clear_screen, clear_race_state, palette 031E7C, in_game, host_record (the host's race_copy record), the stages (screen first_stage-A6) 0, the seat's lamps on (checkpoint_lamp 2, leader_lamp 8), car_select_start started, a fade in, the screen on; 3 the car select (car_select_run goes on to 4); 4 a fade out, entry_countdown_shown cleared, the lamps on; 5 when the fade is over, race ready cleared, silence_and_clear_screen, the screen off (C0001C = 4), the stage's palette (queue_stage_load), the screen's car (object_handlers_346C: player_car_update, its place from screen 320 + player), step 23; 23 once the car has published its state (race_copy published): race_running, the race's objects (object_handlers_3474; the demo's object_handlers_34C4), 24; 24 and 25 a frame each; 26 step 11, the input ring's places to 0, the screen on; 11 a fade in, step 6; 6 when it is over, 7; 7 the race, until its ending is over (flags bit 1, race_car_start_ending): race_running cleared, a fade out, 8; 8 when it is over, the lamps on, 9; 9 the text and the tilemaps off (clear_text_layer, reset_tile_layers), no objects, step 12; 12 the route map: short of the goal (race_copy finished clear) the sound off, the song title Meaning of the Light (start_song_title with 003504) and sound 0B; palette 031E84, route_map_update (object_handlers_34EC), a fade in; 13 when it is over, 14; 14 the map until START (buttons_pressed bit 6) or route_map_update sets 16; 15 (nothing sets it) 16; 16 with a new course record (course_record_beaten) a fade out and 18, else 21; 18 when the fade is over, no objects, name_entry_update started, 19; 19 a fade in, 20; 20 the name entry (name_entry_update sets 21); 21 set_player_ready and 22; 21 and 22 once every player of the race is ready (players_not_ready): a fade out, fade_out_music, 17; 17 when the fade is over, the game's end: the time played (the backup's seconds less game_start_time) added to the seat's total (701F18 + 4 * index) and kept if the longest (701F20) or the shortest (701F22), a game counted at the stage level reached (screen current_stage_level, 6 at the goal: 701F24 + 2 * it), C00074 = rom_003465 then rom_003467 by index, sound 1E, the sound and the tilemaps off, in_game, screen race_started and unk_318 cleared, the race record's car part cleared (race_record_clear), no objects, race game_over (the screens in the attract modes go to the ranking, race_entries_update), the lamps off, clear_lcd, 28; 28 game mode 5 (the ranking). The demo: 10 silence_and_clear_screen, clear_race_state, the stage and the car model from 0034F4 by attract_car (the stage into screen first_stage, A1, A3, A5), host_record its own record at screen + 490 (demo_player_count 1, demo_players this screen's player, demo_stage), the model into car model and race model, palette 031E88, then as 27; 27 waits until race_copy published is clear, then as 5.
comment course_record_beaten Whether the screen's player has beaten the course's record (zero clear): when the race was finished (race_copy finished), the course (the goal's stage, screen built_stage, less 15) into record_course, and a record when the player came first (screen 320 + player is 0) in a time (screen 410 + 2 * player) under the course's (course_record_times by course).
comment load_course_records Loads the ten courses' records into course_records (6 bytes each: the car's model, the name's three letters, the time in hundredths) from the backup RAM (701F32); a course without one there (its first byte negative) gets its default from 002CC4 (SGA, 5'00"00); the model is kept to its low three bits.
comment silence_and_clear_screen The screen's sound off (reset_screen_sound), then clear_screen.
comment clear_screen Clears what the screen shows: no objects (clear_objects), its tilemaps off in the mixer (reset_tile_layers), no sprites (clear_sprite_group_count, screen sprite_groups) and the text layer cleared (clear_text_layer).
comment clear_race_state Clears the race's state: the race record's car part (race_record_clear), the screen's car block and the 100 bytes after it (300 bytes from player_car), the race's order, places and times (screen + 330, 160 bytes) and the ring of views (screen + 600, 100 bytes).
comment clear_published_car Clears what the screen's car hands on to the race record (player_car_publish's), for the ranking (game_mode_ranking): race published, the next of the ring of eight views (screen view_ring_slot, race view_slot, its address in screen shown_view) with the view's place and angles cleared in it and the car (view_x-z, view_pitch, view_yaw), the wheel effects and hard acceleration (race wheel_effect_sides-53), segment_fraction, segment, road_x, window_segment (race segment_fraction-46), window_offset, segment_remaining, the horizon (screen horizon_line), and the halfword at C8 of current_object (player_car_publish's bounce_height) with race bounce_height; from the ranking current_object is what run_objects left in it, not the car.
comment staff_credits_code_control_bit Sets bit R3 of the controls being read (R1) when the value R2 is E0 or more, or C0 or more when the bit was on in the controls held before (R0): staff_credits_code_read_controls' test for the wheel turned right, the gas and the brake.
comment crc16 Returns the CRC-16 (polynomial 1021) of the R11 bytes at R10, worked out in the top half of a 32-bit register that starts at DEBDEB00, each byte entering its low byte, with eight more shifts after the last. Used for the EEPROM's settings (load_settings, eeprom_write_settings) and the program's signature (signature_checksum).
comment signature_checksum Returns the crc16 of the program's 48-byte signature text at 00010C (OUTRUNNERS EUROPA and its build date, 93.4.21), which the EEPROM keeps in settings_signature so that a new program is noticed (load_settings).
comment load_settings Loads the settings from the EEPROM at start-up (eeprom_read_settings into 20E600-20E67F) and repairs them: a block marked SEGA gets the defaults (0004C0, marked S32A) with eeprom_write_count 100; one without the S32A mark the defaults; with it, a new program (signature_checksum unlike settings_signature) gets the defaults keeping the eeprom_write_count of a sound copy (the settings or their backup, each checked with its crc16); with the same program a bad copy (or a backup unlike the settings) is rewritten from the other, both bad the defaults. Every repair but rewriting a copy also resets the backup RAM's data (bookkeeping_reset); each one saves the result (eeprom_write_settings).
comment count_eeprom_write Counts a save of the settings in eeprom_write_count, up to 10000 (the test mode warns when the EEPROM's limit of 10,000 writes is reached).
comment eeprom_lock Locks the EEPROM against writes (command 00, the 93C46's write disable) and turns the interrupts back on.
comment eeprom_read_settings Reads the EEPROM's 64 words into 20E600-20E67F (settings_magic, settings_signature, the settings from settings_checksum on, their backup from backup_settings_checksum on), with the interrupts off.
comment save_settings Saves the settings into the EEPROM after they changed (the link's, link_take_master_settings; the test mode's, test_mode_exit): eeprom_write_settings_counted, then eeprom_lock.
comment eeprom_write_settings_counted Counts the save (count_eeprom_write) and writes the settings (eeprom_write_settings).
comment eeprom_write_settings Writes the settings into the EEPROM: their checksum (crc16 of the 3A bytes from eeprom_write_count) into settings_checksum, checksum and settings copied over the backup (backup_settings_checksum on), the EEPROM unlocked (command 30, write enable) and its 64 words written from 20E600 (eeprom_write_word). Leaves the interrupts off and the EEPROM unlocked (save_settings locks it).
comment eeprom_read_word Reads the EEPROM word of read command R4 (eeprom_send_command): 18 clock pulses collecting the data out (bit 7 of R12, port F of the second I/O chip), the 16-bit word into R0; then chip select off.
comment eeprom_write_word Writes R0 into the EEPROM word of write command R4 (eeprom_send_command): its 16 bits high first, chip select dropped to start the write, then waits, clocking, until the data out (bit 7 of R12) says it is done.
comment eeprom_send_command Sends command R4 to the EEPROM (a 93C46 on port H of the second I/O chip, R13: bit 7 data in, 6 clock, 5 chip select): chip select, a start bit, then the 8 bits of R4 high first (two of opcode: 2 read, 1 write, 0 with the address's top bits 3 write enable or 0 write disable; six of address), each with a clock pulse.
comment eeprom_begin Starts an EEPROM access: the interrupts off, R13 the EEPROM's lines (C8000E, port H of the second I/O chip), R12 its data out (C8000A, port F, bit 7), R2 the lines' present value.
comment eeprom_set_lines Writes R2 to the EEPROM's lines (R13), with bit 4 set too.
comment eeprom_read_one_word Unused: reads the EEPROM's word R4 (its read command, R4 | 80) into R0 and turns the interrupts back on.
comment reload_settings Unused: reads the settings from the EEPROM again (eeprom_read_settings) and turns the interrupts back on.
comment eeprom_write_one_word Unused: writes R0 into the EEPROM's word R4 (its write command, R4 | 40) without unlocking it first, and turns the interrupts back on.
field screen 40 link_event u8
field screen 45 link_status u8
field screen 46 link_status_set u8
field screen 47 link_status_cleared u8
field screen 48 link_status_changed u8
field screen 80 game_mode u8
field screen 81 mode_step u8
field screen 82 game_step u8
field screen 88 mode_timer u16

# ======== the race's logic, the link between cabinets, the draw modes and the LCD (naming batch H) ========
00AB24  func  distance_offset_from_x

comment distance_offset_from_x Unused (only unk_00AB04 lists it, for octants 1, 2, 5 and 6 of an angle): the distance along angle R0 that has the x distance from R11's x (+4) to the object's field 20 (that distance / the sine of R0), turned to angle R1 by distance_offset_at_angle. Its C has no body: nothing reads the results (R2, R3).
comment distance_offset_from_z Unused (only unk_00AB04 lists it, for octants 0, 3, 4 and 7 of an angle): the distance along angle R0 that has the z distance from R11's z (+8) to the object's field 28 (that distance / the cosine of R0), turned to angle R1 by distance_offset_at_angle. Its C has no body: nothing reads the results (R2, R3).
comment distance_offset_at_angle Unused (the end of distance_offset_from_x and distance_offset_from_z): R2 / R6 as a length along angle R1, its sine part into R2 and its cosine part into R3. Its C has no body: nothing reads the results.
comment wheel_effect_1_none The wheel at road_x plus the model's half track (handling +1A) is within the bounds of both surface entries: no effect (player_car_road_drag's pick, rom_00B70C).
comment wheel_effect_1_surface_1_inner wheel_effect_sides bit 0 and wheel_effect_1 = the effect (+6) of surface_entry_1 past its inner bound (+2): the wheel at road_x plus the half track is past it and no other bound (player_car_road_drag's pick, rom_00B70C).
comment wheel_effect_1_surface_1_outer wheel_effect_sides bit 0 and wheel_effect_1 = the effect (+7) of surface_entry_1 past its outer bound (+4), when the wheel at road_x plus the half track is past it and not past the bounds of surface_entry_2.
comment wheel_effect_1_surface_2_inner wheel_effect_sides bit 0 and wheel_effect_1 = the effect (+6) of surface_entry_2 past its inner bound (+2), when the wheel at road_x plus the half track is past it but not past its outer bound.
comment wheel_effect_1_surface_2_outer wheel_effect_sides bit 0 and wheel_effect_1 = the effect (+7) of surface_entry_2 past its outer bound (+4): for the wheel at road_x plus the half track it comes before every other.
comment wheel_effect_2_none The wheel at road_x less the model's half track is within the bounds of both surface entries: no effect (player_car_road_drag's pick, rom_00B74C).
comment wheel_effect_2_surface_1_inner wheel_effect_sides bit 1 and wheel_effect_2 = the effect (+6) of surface_entry_1 past its inner bound (+2), when the wheel at road_x less the half track is past it but not past its outer bound.
comment wheel_effect_2_surface_1_outer wheel_effect_sides bit 1 and wheel_effect_2 = the effect (+7) of surface_entry_1 past its outer bound (+4): for the wheel at road_x less the half track it comes before every other.
comment wheel_effect_2_surface_2_inner wheel_effect_sides bit 1 and wheel_effect_2 = the effect (+6) of surface_entry_2 past its inner bound (+2), when the wheel at road_x less the half track passes no bound of surface_entry_1 nor the outer one of surface_entry_2.
comment wheel_effect_2_surface_2_outer wheel_effect_sides bit 1 and wheel_effect_2 = the effect (+7) of surface_entry_2 past its outer bound (+4), when the wheel at road_x less the half track passes no bound of surface_entry_1.
comment network_check_again The network check after the test mode: network_check, or in ISDN mode (isdn_mode) the link board off for a moment, the link's variables (from machine_count), the board's memory at 800004 and 800600, this machine's two race records and all the players' records cleared, then network_check_isdn.
comment network_check The network check at boot: the link board off (801000), the link's variables, the board's first bytes and all the race records cleared; with the STAND-ALONE setting (stand_alone into link_off) only network_check_stand_alone. Else it shows NETWORK CHECK (MASTER, SLAVE or ISDN) and BOARD CHECK, tests the board's memory (network_check_board_memory), writes its greeting V70 and turns the board on, waits for it (network_check_wait_board), checks the board's answer Z80 (network_check_greeting), waits again, shows the board's self-test (network_check_four_ics); in ISDN mode goes on to network_check_isdn; else gives the board this machine's role (link_master at 800004), waits, shows the self-test again (network_check_five_ics), waits for the board's flag (801002 bit 7) with CHECK NETWORK STATUS blinking, gives it this machine's number (800002 to 800E20) and FF at 800006, shows CHECK TRANS END until the board's 800008 is set (link_lost if 80000C is), and goes on to network_check_join. The test button (test_mode_on) gives it up at any wait (link_failed FF).
comment network_check_join Takes the link from the board: machine_count from its byte at 800000, player_count twice it, this machine's number from 800002, the board's flag 801002 set; with no machine on the link (and not in ISDN mode) network_check_stand_alone; else the two screens' car numbers from the cabinet ID (cabinet_id * 2 and + 1, into screen car_number and race car_number), then network_check_result with this machine's number.
comment network_check_isdn The network check's ISDN mode: the link board off for a moment, 1 into its byte at 800014 (0 in network mode), its flag clear, the board on, network_check_wait_isdn, then network_check_join.
comment link_lost The link has failed (the board's error byte 80000C, seen by network_check, link_receive_records or link_send_records): link_failed 1, then network_check_stand_alone (which shows COMMUNICATION FAILED. for 128 frames).
comment network_check_stand_alone Sets the machine up alone: link_off, one machine and two players (machine_count, player_count), car numbers 0 and 1 for the two screens (screen and race car_number), then network_check_result as machine 1.
comment network_check_result Sets up the machine's two players from its number R0 (1-4): both race records' machine, their players ((R0 - 1) * 2 and + 1, also screen player), the screens' race_copy (race_copy_offsets by player: 20FC00 + 80 each) and race (20FB00, 20FB80); machine 1 also puts its settings (advertise_sound, steering_wheel_reaction, coins_per_credit, first_chute_multiplier, bonus_adder, coin_setting, free_play, game_difficulty, time_difficulty[0-5]) at 03-10 of its first record (the master_ fields; second_chute_multiplier is not sent) and its course records (the backup's, 701F32) at 30. Then NETWORK CHECK and the result for 128 frames: COMMUNICATION FAILED. (link_failed), THIS MACHINE IS ISDN MODE., THIS MACHINE IS STAND-ALONE., or SLAVE / MASTER CONTROLLER (the board's 800004) with COMMUNICATION SUCCESS. and THIS MACHINE ID IS n (the board's 800002); the text cleared.
comment network_check_board_memory Tests the link board's memory (network_check_memory_test over 7FF of its bytes); on a fault the screens off, CONNECTION ERROR and network_check_error.
comment network_check_memory_test A memory test: eight passes (seed 01, 02, ... 80), each writing a pattern from the seed into R1 bytes (one a halfword) from R10, reading them back and gathering the bits that differ into R0's low byte (0: no fault); CHECK DUALPORT RAM flips on the status line after each pass (network_check_toggle_memory_text).
comment network_check_wait_board Waits for the link board's flag (801002 bit 7), WAIT blinking, for up to E10 frames (two minutes, link_wait_frames): then the screens off, COMMUNICATION TIME OUT and network_check_error; the test button gives the check up (link_failed FF); the status line cleared after.
comment network_check_wait_isdn ISDN mode: waits, WAIT blinking, until the link board's byte 800000 (its machine count) is set, then 60 frames more (link_wait_frames); the test button gives the check up (link_failed FF); the status line cleared after.
comment network_check_greeting Checks the greetings on the link board: V70 (this program's, at 800000) and Z80 (the board's answer, at 800010); otherwise the screens off, CONNECTION ERROR and network_check_error.
comment network_check_five_ics The link board's self-test (its byte 80000C), the second time: clear, IC17, IC23, IC11, IC10 and IC9 GOOD (network_check_show_ics); else the screens off, COMMUNICATION BOARD ERROR and network_check_four_ics, which, the byte still set, shows four of the ICs and halts there (the five after it only show if the byte clears meanwhile), then network_check_halt.
comment network_check_four_ics The link board's self-test (its byte 80000C), the first time: clear, IC17, IC23, IC11 and IC10 GOOD (network_check_show_ics); else the screens off, COMMUNICATION BOARD ERROR with each of the four GOOD or BAD, the screens on, and network_check_halt.
comment network_check_show_ics Shows R0 of the link board's ICs (IC17, IC23, IC11, IC10, IC9), each GOOD or BAD by its bit in R2 (rom_00E4D0).
comment network_check_blink_text Blinks a text record (at current_object: its cell, attribute and characters) by flipping it into the text layer every 16th call (network_check_blink_counter): WAIT, CHECK NETWORK STATUS, NETWORK ERROR.
comment network_check_clear_status_line Clears row 24 of the text layer, where the network check's waits blink their texts.
comment network_check_toggle_memory_text Flips CHECK DUALPORT RAM on or off on the status line (row 24), once each pass of network_check_memory_test.
comment network_check_error Shows the error R19 (a text list: CONNECTION ERROR, COMMUNICATION TIME OUT...) on a cleared text layer with the screens on, then halts like network_check_halt.
comment network_check_halt Blinks NETWORK ERROR until the test button (test_mode_on) is pressed; then link_failed FF and the network check is given up (it returns past its caller).
comment link_take_master_settings After the network check (unless the test button is down): sends and receives the race records (link_send_records, 5 frames, link_receive_records; in ISDN mode once the board's 8006E1 is set, link_isdn_receive_records), keeps each player's car number (race car_number into car_numbers), takes the first player's (the master's) course records into the backup (701F32 with its checksum, 011418, and the copy at 701FAE) and into course_records (001D84), and adopts its settings (record +03-10 into advertise_sound, steering_wheel_reaction, coins_per_credit, first_chute_multiplier, bonus_adder, coin_setting, free_play, game_difficulty, time_difficulty[0-5]; second_chute_multiplier is not taken; 011018 and 01139E when any of the coin settings changes, all saved by 00036B when any changes).
comment race_entries_update The entries to a race, each frame. Player 0's screen keeps them in its record: once every entry is taken (it means to wait for each record's entry_serial_seen, but compares player 0's own each time), a player's entry_request not yet accepted (accepted_players) is accepted: entrant = that player, entry_serial + 1, and the first one starts entry_frames at 600 (rom_00E614), cut to 40 once every player is in; entry_frames counting down to 0 closes the entries (entrant FF, entry_serial + 1, accepted_players cleared, 1 to the link board's 80000A). Every screen: when some player's race is over (race game_over) an idle screen (game steps 1-5) goes to step 5 (cleared_on_race_over cleared), and while player 0's entry_frames runs to step 7 (entry_invitation_step); each new entry_serial of player 0's record (entry_serial_seen) adds its entrant to the screen's entrants (entrant_count), or the close clears them (and entry_accepted); the screen's own player accepted: its entry_request cleared, entry_accepted, the first entrant into host_player and is_host when that is itself; the host keeps racer_count and race_order in its record from the entrants.
comment race_progress_update The race's progress, each frame of a race (screen in_game). The host (is_host) runs it in its record: once every racer has taken the last pass (pass_seen = pass_count), a racer whose stage_count changed (against counted_stages) has passed a checkpoint (stage_count - 1): pass_player, pass_checkpoint, pass_order (counted_passes), pass_branch (the side of the road it was on: race road_x bit 15), pass_time (race_time in hundredths plus the order), pass_count + 1; the first to pass a checkpoint before the goal adds the time of the stage its branch leads to (rom_00E5B3 by rom_00E5D3[stage * 2 + branch]) plus the setting for that checkpoint (master_time_difficulty) to time_left. Once the race has started (race_started) race_time + 34 and time_left - 1 a frame, and one pass of sorting race_order: the finished (race finished) first by finish_time, then by distance (race segment_fraction-43). Every screen from the host's record (host_record): the leader's segment and stage (leader_window_segment, leader_stage), each player's place (places, its own in place); each new pass: pass_seen (screen and race), checkpoints_passed, checkpoint_passes, the order and the time by player and by order (checkpoint_orders, checkpoint_order, checkpoint_times_by_player, checkpoint_times); the first car past a fork decides the route: forks_decided + 1, its branch in route_branches and the stage it leads to in route_stages.
comment entry_invitation_step Game step 7, an idle seat while another one's game takes entries (race_entries_update sends it here), by screen game_mode's byte 2: 0 screen fade_kind = 0 and unk_94 bit 0; 1 once that bit has cleared: the seat's sound channel (012004), the screen's objects (011C60), layers (01070C), sprite count (010889) and text layer (01076A) set afresh, its race record's race part cleared (race_record_clear), not the demo (unk_90 bit 8), the wheel motor off (wheel_motor), both of the seat's lamps blinking (bytes C0-C3 and C8-CB of its entry at checkpoint_lamp); 2 starts object_handlers_DF74 (entry_countdown_start, credit_display_update, coin_prompt_update): the invitation to join with the entry time left; 3 the seat's screen on (its I/O chip's CNT, 6 or 86), unk_153 = 1, unk_94 bit 0; 4 once player 0's entry_frames is over: the sound channel again (012004), step 2, the lamps off, the LCD cleared (clear_lcd).
comment race_record_clear Clears bytes 30-7F of the screen's race record (the race's part: the car's published state, the checkpoint passes, the times), from the zeros at 100800.
comment course_records_update Takes the players' new course records, each frame: when a player's record_serial changes (against name_entries_seen), its time (finish_time) on its course (record_course) at or under the course's record (course_records, 6 bytes a course: model, the three letters of the name, the time) replaces it with its record_model and record_name (the first letter) and record_name_rest (the other two), also in the two copies in the backup (701F32 with its checksum, 011418, and 701FAE).
comment link_receive_records Takes the race records of all the players into 20FC00 (80 bytes each, by player), each frame: alone (link_off, link_failed) this machine's own two (20FB00, 20FB80); in ISDN mode the eight from 21E000; on the link the eight from the link board (link_copy_records_in from 800020, or 800720 when its byte 80000E is set), or link_lost when its error byte 80000C is set.
comment link_isdn_receive_records ISDN mode: copies the eight race records from the link board (800600) to 21E000, where link_receive_records takes them; the link interrupt (int_vector_44) does it on the board's command 3.
comment link_copy_records_in Copies R1 race records from the link board's memory at R10 (a byte in each halfword) to R11: their first 70 bytes, 80 apart.
comment link_send_records Sends this machine's two race records to the link board, each frame (not in ISDN mode, nor alone): their first 70 bytes to 800E20 and 800F00 (a byte in each halfword), then FF to its byte 800006; link_lost when its error byte 80000C is set. link_wait_frames from link_wait_frames_start (which nothing sets).
comment draw_object_between_segments Draw mode 2: the object between the road's segment at its position (field 1C, counted from the view drawn: race_copy window_segment) and the next, by the fraction in field 28 (80ths), offset across and up by fields 20 and 24 scaled by each segment's scale (draw_object_at_scale). Its size, meant as the two segments' scales by the fraction, multiplies them by the second point's offsets instead (R4 and R5 used twice): a slip in the game's code; nothing in the scenarios draws with it.
comment draw_object_from_road_edge Draw mode 5: the object on the road's surface between its segment (field 1C) and the next by the fraction in field 28, across at field 20 from the road's edge on side field 3F (the segment's half width times the side), its size the two segments' scales by the fraction shifted by scale_shift (draw_object_at_shifted_scale).
comment draw_object_from_road_edge_unshifted Draw mode 7: as draw_object_from_road_edge, at the size of scale_shift 0 whatever the object's.
comment draw_object_from_road_edge_doubled Draw mode B (the traffic cars'): as draw_object_from_road_edge, at twice the size of draw_object_from_road_edge_unshifted (scale_shift 1) whatever the object's.
comment draw_object_at_car_window_segment Draw mode 4: as draw_object_at_segment, but the segment counted from the screen car's own window_segment instead of the view drawn (race_copy window_segment).
comment draw_object_at_segment Draw mode 9: the object at the road's segment of its position (field 1C, counted from the view drawn: race_copy window_segment), offset across and up by fields 20 and 24 scaled by the segment's scale, its size that scale shifted by scale_shift; not drawn when the segment is behind or not projected.
comment draw_object_at_car_window_segment_on_road Draw mode 3: as draw_object_at_car_window_segment, on the road's surface (no up offset).
comment draw_object_at_segment_scale Draws the object at R0, R1 at the projected segment R11's own scale (draw_object_at_shifted_scale).
comment draw_object_at_shifted_scale Draws the object at R0, R1 at the scale R2 shifted by its scale_shift (and >> 4) (draw_object_at_scale).
comment draw_object_at_scale Places a road object projected at R0, R1 with size R2 (both scales): x and y; off the screen (x beyond 1A0 or -1A0, y beyond 80 or -160) back to draw_object's caller undrawn; the depth_layer of the projected segment R11 (+8), then put_object_sprites.
comment draw_object_on_screen Draw mode 0: the object at x, y on the screen, less the screen's scroll (screen sprite_origin_x, unk_16), at its width_scale and height_scale (put_object_sprites).
comment put_object_sprites Writes the object's frame (animation, frame) into the screen's sprite list at R10 and its copy at + 3000, a sprite of 8 halfwords for each piece: its word flipped by flags bits 6-7, its size from 0x122B74 by size_step scaled by R2 (height) and R3 (width), placed at R0, R1 plus its offsets scaled the same way (signs flipped by the flags: rom_00F13C, rom_00F140), its palette word from rom_032BA4 by palette; R10 past them.
comment lcd_start Starts the cabinets' LCDs at boot (both, through port G of the two I/O chips): the 4-bit start (command nibbles 3, 3, 3, 2), display on (0C), SEGA Enterprises as both screens' text (lcd_text), command 40 then the eight custom characters (rom_018B4E: the DJ's face among them); the screens' lamps off (screen button_lamps), lcd_restart_frames E10.
comment lcd_write_character Writes the data byte R0 to both LCDs, its high nibble then its low one (lcd_write_data_nibble), with pauses.
comment lcd_write_data_nibble Writes the high nibble of R0 to both LCDs as data (port G of the two I/O chips, inverted: the data lines, register select set, then the enable strobe bit 3 dropped).
comment lcd_command_nibble_after_pause A pause, then lcd_write_command_nibble.
comment lcd_write_command_nibble Writes the high nibble of R0 to both LCDs as a command (register select clear), then drops the enable strobe (bit 3).
comment lcd_reset After the test mode: both screens' LCD text back to SEGA Enterprises (lcd_text), their lamps off (screen button_lamps), and lcd_restart_frames 0 so that the next VBLANK starts the LCDs again.
comment lcd_refresh Each VBLANK: counts lcd_restart_frames down and, when it runs out, has the LCDs started again (lcd_init_step 2C, E10 VBLANKs to the next, a minute); else, unless a nibble is being written (lcd_busy), after a byte cut in half (an odd lcd_send_step) writes one more command nibble (0 or 4) to keep the LCDs in step, takes both screens' lcd_text into lcd_send_buffer_1 and lcd_send_buffer_2 and starts sending them (lcd_send_step 0).
comment lcd_send_next_nibble One nibble to the LCDs each timer interrupt (int_vector_43), with the other bits of port G (port_g_outputs, the lamps): while lcd_init_step counts, the start sequence (rom_018B21, from the end), then idle (lcd_send_step 22); else by lcd_send_step: the cursor home command (80), then the 16 characters of lcd_send_buffer_1 to screen 1's LCD and of lcd_send_buffer_2 to screen 2's, a nibble at a time; lcd_busy while it writes the port.
comment lcd_update The screen's LCD, each frame. With the DJ's face on (dj_face) its first two characters are the face, its mouth the next shape of dj_mouth_shapes every third frame (dj_mouth_frames; rom_018AE8, pairs of custom characters), closed when the string ends (a negative byte); the rest of the line (all 16 without the face) scrolls a text: a new lcd_scroll_request clears the line and starts lcd_scroll, which shifts the text in from the right a character every 5 frames (lcd_scroll_frames; ` shows custom character 7), then shifts spaces in until the text has gone (lcd_scroll_step 0-3).
field screen 01 player u8
field screen 02 car_number u8
field screen 300 accepted_players u8
field screen 308 host_record u32 race
field screen 30C host_player u8
field screen 30F entrant_count u8
field screen 310 entrants u8
field screen 318 is_host u8
field screen 319 entry_accepted u8
field screen 31C race_started u8
field screen 31F place u8
field screen 320 places u8
field screen 328 name_entries_seen u8
field screen 330 counted_stages u8
